A physically separated noise model treats gyroscope and accelerometer channels independently: the accelerometer provides translational twist via gravity-compensated integration, yielding a measurement covariance that scales with the sample interval in exact analogy with process noise discretisation; a state-dependent Coriolis noise term captures gyroscope noise propagating through the nonlinear dynamics, vanishing at rest and growing with twist magnitude. The filter is structured as a modular chain of per-link IEKFs in which the predicted covariance of each link depends on its predecessor only through the Adjoint-transformed posterior, giving linear computational cost in link count. Exponential ultimate boundedness in mean square is established via a Lie algebra Lyapunov function, with per-link bounds chained through the Adjoint operator norm to yield a stability certificate that is modular and scalable to arbitrary chain length. Numerical results validate the design.

</details>


### [147] [Urban Deceleration Behavior Modes Under Scene Context: An Early-Kinematic Classifier from Argoverse 2 Multi-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00027)
*Eni Solomon Laughter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于Argoverse 2数据集提取1219次城市减速事件，通过19维运动学特征和K-means聚类识别出四种稳定减速模式（预期软减速62.8%、反应性接近30.6%、刹车式急动4.8%、异常1.8%），发现跟车年龄是主要调节因素，场景几何和弱势道路使用者接近影响可忽略，早期窗口急动度是预测信号主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市减速是跟车行为中研究最广泛但缺乏系统分类的行为。最近的感知型自动驾驶数据集提供了基于轨迹的模式发现机会，因此本文旨在对城市减速行为进行系统性的模态识别与分类。

Method: 从Argoverse 2 Sensor数据集的234条城市驾驶日志中提取1219次持续减速事件，将每个事件编码为19维运动学特征向量，使用K-means聚类结合bootstrap稳定性分析发现行为模式，并通过11个场景上下文变量量化调节作用。使用HistGradientBoosting分类器从事件前1.0秒预测模式归属。

Result: 发现四种稳定模式（ARI=0.897）：预期软减速62.8%、反应性接近30.6%、刹车式急动4.8%、异常1.8%。仅跟车年龄有中等效应（ε²=0.085），场景几何和弱势道路使用者接近度影响可忽略。早期事件分类器在1.0秒时达到macro-F1=0.758，场景上下文比纯运动学提升+0.059 F1。模式在中速行驶中稳定（ARI=0.817），低速时不稳定（ARI=0.166）。

Conclusion: 城市减速行为可由少量稳定运动学模式结构化，早期窗口急动度是主要预测信号，跟车年龄是主要上下文调节因素。

Abstract: Urban deceleration is one of the most empirically studied yet least taxonomically organized behaviors in car-following research. Recent perception-equipped autonomous-vehicle datasets enable trajectory-anchored mode discovery. We extract 1,219 sustained deceleration events from 234 urban driving logs of the Argoverse 2 Sensor dataset, encode each event in a 19-dimensional kinematic feature vector, discover behavioral modes via K-means clustering with bootstrap stability analysis, and quantify modulation by eleven scene-context variables. A HistGradientBoosting classifier predicts mode membership from the first 1.0 s of each event. Four stable modes emerge with a bootstrap Adjusted Rand Index of 0.897 across 50 resamples: anticipatory soft (62.8%), reactive closing (30.6%), brake-like jerk (4.8%), and an outlier category (1.8%). Only pair age shows a medium effect (epsilon^2 = 0.085); scene geometry and vulnerable-road-user proximity show negligible effects. The early-event classifier achieves macro-F1 = 0.758 at 1.0 s, with scene context contributing +0.059 F1 over kinematics alone. Modes are regime-invariant in medium-speed driving (ARI = 0.817) but regime-dependent at low speed (ARI = 0.166). A small set of stable kinematic modes structures urban deceleration; early-window jerk dominates predictive signal; and pair age is the primary contextual modulator.

</details>


### [148] [Trajectory Learning with Graph Representations for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00028)
*Berke Kartal,Burcu Kilic,Yigit Yildirim,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图注意力网络的模仿学习框架，通过时空动态建模实现社交合规导航，在仿真和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法同时处理真实数据中的空间和时间特征：强化学习需要手工设计奖励函数，将社交行为简化为静态标准；模仿学习缺乏社交交互建模且存在误差累积问题。

Method: 提出模仿学习框架：1) 图注意力辅助网络，通过关注行人编码群状态表示社交上下文；2) 导航模块，结合编码状态预测和轨迹级学习目标捕捉时间动态并缓解误差累积。

Result: 在仿真和真实数据集上，该框架在各种社交指标上均优于已有的数据驱动基线方法。

Conclusion: 同时建模时空动态的模仿学习能有效提升移动机器人在人群中的社交合规导航能力。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots are expected to exhibit socially compliant navigation for minimizing pedestrian disturbance. While capturing social interactions and incorporating pedestrian motion estimations into decision-making are beneficial for compliance, prior methods fail to address both spatial and temporal characteristics present in real-world data. Reinforcement Learning offers high capability, but it requires hand-crafted reward functions that reduce social behavior to static criteria, limiting its ability to reproduce patterns that exist in real pedestrian behavior. Imitation Learning offers direct training from real-world data but lacks modeling of social interactions and suffers from error accumulation. To this end, we propose an imitation learning framework that leverages spatiotemporal dynamics for socially compliant navigation. To represent social context based on interactions, we introduce a graph-based auxiliary network that encodes crowd states by attending to pedestrians. In addition, we present a navigation module that captures temporal dynamics and mitigates error accumulations by incorporating encoded state predictions and employing a trajectory-level learning objective. Our framework outperforms established data-driven baselines on simulation and a real-world dataset across diverse social metrics.

</details>


### [149] [Memory-Native Non-Terrestrial Networks for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00029)
*Chengyang Li,Yikun Wang,Jiahui He,Yujie Wan,Shuai Wang,Yuan Wu,Yik-Chung Wu,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MemNTN范式，通过双记忆架构（物理记忆+数字记忆）让非地面网络具备长期记忆能力，显著优于传统无状态NTN方案，在卫星具身问答（SEQA）任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NTN协议是无记忆的，仅依赖局部信道条件和瞬时服务需求做决策，无法适应高度动态、资源受限、拓扑变化且任务导向的具身智能环境。

Method: 提出MemNTN范式，建立双记忆架构（物理记忆=世界状态，数字记忆=历史网络经验），设计记忆的获取、压缩、估值、更新和利用机制，实现跨层记忆原生决策。

Result: 在卫星具身问答（SEQA）实验中，MemNTN显著优于传统无状态NTN和地面方法。

Conclusion: 引入长期上下文的记忆机制能有效提升NTN在具身智能场景下的性能，实现跨层优化的记忆原生决策。

Abstract: Non-terrestrial networks (NTN) provide ubiquitous connectivity for embodied intelligence (EI), enabling robots in wilderness to leverage cloud resources or report critical information to remote centers. However, the synergy is nontrivial due to the highly-dynamic, resource-constrained, topology-varying, and task-oriented environment. Existing memoryless NTN protocols become inefficient, since the decisions are driven by local channel conditions and instantaneous service demands. To address these limitations, this paper proposes the memory-native NTN (MemNTN) paradigm that leverages long-horizon contexts for memory augmented system optimization. To realize this paradigm shift, we establish a dual-memory architecture that distinguishes between physical memory representing the state of the world and digital memory encoding historical network experience. We develop memory acquisition, compression, valuation, update, and utilization mechanisms that facilitate cross-layer, memory-native decision-making, spanning from the physical and access layers up to the network and application layers. Experiments in satellite embodied question answering (SEQA) demonstrate that the proposed MemNTN significantly outperforms conventional stateless NTN and terrestrial approaches.

</details>


### [150] [A Unified Benchmark for RCM-Constrained Visual Servoing: Modeling-Controller Interaction and Robustness Analysis in Laparoscopic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00030)
*Jing Zhang,Mengtang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源仿真框架，统一了三种RCM建模方法和六种IBVS控制架构，用于系统评估RCM约束下的视觉伺服控制，揭示了建模与控制器交互中的关键结构敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCM约束方法（特别是控制方法）虽灵活且成本低，但由于缺乏统一可复现的基准，不同RCM公式和IBVS框架的系统性比较十分困难。

Method: 构建了开源仿真框架，在统一的速度级公式下集成了三种代表性RCM建模方法和六种IBVS控制架构，并通过结构化案例研究进行评估。

Result: 揭示了模型与控制器交互导致的关键结构敏感性，包括切平面定义、约束维度、开环与闭环执行方式的影响，以及在运动学奇异性附近的鲁棒性差异。

Conclusion: 该框架为RCM约束的视觉伺服研究提供了可复现的基础，所有资源已开源，有助于推动该领域的标准化评估与发展。

Abstract: In robot-assisted laparoscopic minimally invasive surgery (MIS), accurate enforcement of the remote center of motion (RCM) constraint is critical for safe and stable automatic field-of-view (FoV) adjustment. Although control-based RCM strategies are widely adopted due to their flexibility and cost-effectiveness, systematic comparison of different RCM formulations and image-based visual servoing (IBVS) frameworks remains challenging due to the lack of a unified and reproducible benchmark. This paper presents an open-source simulation framework integrating three representative RCM modeling approaches and six IBVS-based control architectures within a unified velocity-level formulation, enabling controlled and consistent evaluation. Through structured case studies, the framework reveals key structural sensitivities arising from modeling and controller interactions, including the impact of tangent-plane definition, constraint dimensionality, open- versus closed-loop enforcement, and robustness near kinematic singularities. All resources are released and demostrations are provided in the supplementary video, providing a reproducible foundation for RCM-constrained visual servoing research.

</details>


### [151] [Joint Discovery of Object and Action Symbols through Effect Prediction for Robotic Manipulation Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00031)
*Burcu Kilic,Berke Kartal,Fatih Dogangun,Erhan Oztop,Emre Ugur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过二进制瓶颈层联合发现高级操作原语和物体类别的模型，利用多模态预测（物体运动、接触、力反馈）进行离散规划，并在桌面重定位与堆叠任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法或依赖视觉外观（无法区分外观相似但行为不同的物体），或局限于预定义动作，缺乏从交互效果中联合发现操作原语与物体类别的能力。

Method: 提出包含二进制瓶颈层的模型，从随机交互数据中预测多模态结果（运动、接触、力反馈），联合学习高层操作基元与物体类别符号；利用离散规划方法结合中间效果轨迹实现精确低层控制；通过少量交互效果与已学习符号对比实现基于行为的少样本泛化。

Result: 在桌面重定位与堆叠任务中，该效果驱动的规划方法在规划精度上超越了最先进方法及基于视觉的替代方案，且对未见过的物体具有泛化能力。

Conclusion: 基于交互效果而非视觉相似性的符号化表示与规划方法能有效提升机器人操作规划的精度与泛化能力。

Abstract: To perform complex manipulation planning, autonomous robots are required to abstract continuous, high-dimensional sensorimotor interactions into discrete object and action representations. Earlier work either categorized objects based on visual appearances, which fails to distinguish objects that appear similar but behave differently, or based on effects under interaction, but was limited to predefined actions. To address these limitations, we propose a model that jointly discovers high-level manipulation primitives and object categories through a binary bottleneck layer, trained to predict multi-modal outcomes, including object motion, contact, and force feedback, from random interaction data. Building on these discovered binary representations, we leverage a discrete planning method that uses intermediate steps in the predicted effect trajectory to enable partial action executions for precise low-level control. Additionally, we evaluate our framework's generalization capabilities on novel objects by assigning object categories through comparing a small number of interaction effects with the predicted effects of learned object symbols, enabling few-shot generalization based on behavior rather than visual similarity. We conduct experiments on tabletop repositioning and stacking tasks, and confirm that our effect-driven planning approach outperforms both a state-of-the-art method and a visual-based alternative in planning precision across seen and novel objects.

</details>


### [152] [Learning Dexterous Manipulation Using Contact Wrench Guidance From Human Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00033)
*Xinghao Zhu,Zixi Liu,Shalin Jain,Chenran Li,Milad Noori,Huihua Zhao,John Welsh,Michael Andres Lin,Wei Liu,Tingwu Wang,Xingye Da,Zhengyi Luo,Vishal Kulkarni,Naema Bhatti,Yuke Zhu,Linxi Fan,Bowen Wen,Danfei Xu,Soha Pouya,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CHORD是一个利用人类示教通过接触力/力矩空间引导来训练灵巧操作策略的强化学习框架，在超过1800个任务上取得82.12%的成功率，并成功迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类示教数据丰富但难以直接迁移为机器人策略，尤其是在接触丰富的灵巧操作任务中。现有方法在可扩展性和泛化性上存在局限。

Method: 以物体为中心的接触力/力矩空间引导（Contact Wrench Space Guidance），将人类和机器人运动统一表示为对物体施加的力和力矩，通过瞬时运动相似性度量来引导强化学习。同时构建了包含4739个双手灵巧操作任务的大规模仿真基准。

Result: 在1831个基准任务上平均成功率82.12%；从仅手部/第三人称示教泛化到全身操作成功率90.77%；策略在真实世界的开环和闭环设置下均可迁移。

Conclusion: 基于接触力/力矩空间的示教引导方法有效提升了灵巧操作强化学习的可扩展性和泛化能力，能够实现从人类示教到机器人策略的跨场景迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous robot manipulation can benefit from the abundance of human demonstrations, but transferring such demonstrations to robot policies remains challenging. We present Contact Wrench Guidance from Human Demonstration in Robotic Dexterous Manipulation (CHORD), a framework for long-horizon manipulation of rigid and articulated objects with reinforcement learning. The key idea is object-centric contact wrench space guidance: we represent human and robot motions by the forces and torques they can induce on the object, enabling similarity to be measured by the induced instantaneous motions. This guidance makes reinforcement learning more scalable for contact-rich dexterous manipulation. We further introduce a large-scale simulation benchmark with 4,739 bimanual dexterous manipulation tasks, constructed from motion-capture datasets and reconstructed in-house videos. Evaluated on 1,831 benchmark tasks, CHORD achieves an average success rate of 82.12%, demonstrating strong scalability. CHORD also generalizes to whole-body manipulation from hand-only and third-person demonstrations, achieving a 90.77% success rate, and the learned policies transfer to the real world in both open-loop and closed-loop settings.

</details>


### [153] [Optimal any-angle path planning in static and dynamic environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00065)
*Yiyuan Zou,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对栅格上的任意角度路径规划问题，提出了两种保持最优性的加速技术（椭圆前向扩展和视野方法），并基于此开发了Zeta*（静态）和Zeta*-SIPP（动态）算法，在动态环境中比现有最优规划器快20倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任意角度路径规划算法大多无法在动态障碍物环境下保证最优解，亟需一种既能保证最优性又能高效计算的方法。

Method: 1) 椭圆前向扩展（利用椭圆邻域限制搜索空间）；2) 视野方法（替代传统视线检测加速可见性检查）；3) 逆向扫描和正向扫描技术（分别从开放节点和封闭节点建立视觉连接）。基于这些技术开发了Zeta*（静态）和Zeta*-SIPP（动态）算法。

Result: Zeta*与当前最优的Anya算法性能相当；Zeta*-SIPP在动态环境中比最优规划器TO-AA-SIPP快20倍以上。

Conclusion: 本文识别了实现最优任意角度路径规划的关键要素，并提出了一种统一的方法，可适用于静态和动态等多种环境。

Abstract: Any-angle path planning extends traditional graph-based path planning by allowing movement between any pair of vertices, rather than being restricted by predefined edges. It can find straighter and shorter paths in continuous space with graphs, making it particularly suitable for navigation in open areas such as airspaces, warehouses, and oceans. Many any-angle path-planning algorithms have been proposed, but only a few can guarantee optimal solutions, especially in the presence of dynamic obstacles. To address this challenge, this article focuses on optimal any-angle path planning on grids and introduces two general techniques that accelerate computation while preserving optimality in both static and dynamic environments: 1) elliptical forward expansion, which leverages ellipse-based neighborhoods to restrict the search space, and 2) field of view, which replaces traditional line-of-sight methods to speed up visibility checks. To integrate these two techniques, inverted and forward scanning are introduced. Inverted scanning establishes visual connections from open nodes, whereas forward scanning initiates scans from closed nodes. Building on the proposed techniques, Zeta* and Zeta*-SIPP are developed for static and dynamic environments respectively. Zeta*, when combined with forward scanning, is similar to the state-of-the-art algorithm Anya and attains comparable performance. Unlike Anya, Zeta* can be readily extended to other settings, such as dynamic environments (e.g., Zeta*-SIPP). Zeta*-SIPP, with either scanning method, is more than 20 times faster than the corresponding state-of-the-art optimal planner TO-AA-SIPP. Overall, this research identifies the key requirements for achieving optimal any-angle path planning and introduces a unified approach suitable for different environments.

</details>


### [154] [Learning Expert Strategy for Autonomous Robotic Endovascular Intervention via Decoupled Procedural Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00066)
*Yanxi Chen,Tianliang Yao,Shaolong Tang,Jiyuan Zhao,Hengyu Hu,Zhaoxing Li,Antonio J. Sánchez Egea,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将强化学习高层策略与专家知识低层执行解耦的自主血管介入导航框架，在仿真和真实机器人平台上实现了>96%导航成功率、29.3%操作步骤减少和13%轨迹方差降低，显著提升了手术的安全性和标准化水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血管介入手术对操作精度要求极高，传统强化学习方法虽能实现一定自主性，但难以满足显式约束满足和安全保障，导致手术质量不一致。

Method: 将自主决策解耦为两层：上层通过强化学习策略生成全局导航意图（战略决策），下层通过专家知识模块进行精化执行，确保机器人动作严格遵循专家操作规范、实时运动学限制和血管安全约束。

Result: 导航成功率>96%，操作步骤减少29.3%，轨迹方差降低13%，在仿真和真实机器人平台上均优于基线策略，达到专家级操作水平。

Conclusion: 该解耦框架能有效提升自主血管介入导航的可预测性、安全性和一致性，有望推动机器人血管介入手术的标准化进程。

Abstract: Endovascular interventions are high-stakes procedures requiring precise device operation within complex and tortuous vascular anatomies. Autonomous endovascular navigation has the potential to standardize procedural quality and reduce the performance variability inherent in manual operation. Although Reinforcement Learning (RL) approaches have demonstrated promise in enabling autonomy in endovascular intervention, they often struggle with explicit constraint satisfaction and safety guarantees. To address these challenges, a learning-based expert strategy is introduced, enhancing procedural consistency in autonomous endovascular intervention by explicitly decoupling high-level strategic decision-making from low-level procedural execution. The proposed framework replicates the expert clinical decision-making process: a strategic RL policy generates global navigation intents, which are subsequently refined through an expert-informed execution module. This module ensures that robot movements strictly adhere to expert operational norms, real-time kinematic limits, and vessel safety constraints. Experimental evaluation across high-fidelity 3D simulations and a real-world robotic platform demonstrates that the proposed framework not only outperforms baseline policies but also effectively replicates expert-level proficiency. The framework achieves a high navigation success rate (> 96%) and a 29.3% reduction in operational steps, which translates to enhanced operative efficiency and minimized device-vessel interaction. Furthermore, a 13% reduction in trajectory variance indicates superior procedural standardization, aligning autonomous behavior with established clinical norms. These results underscore its potential to enhance the predictability, safety, and consistency of robotic endovascular interventions.

</details>


### [155] [AD-MPCC: Adaptive Differentiable Model Predictive Contouring Control for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00141)
*Nam T. Nguyen,Binh Nguyen,Ahmad Amine,Thanh Vo-Duy,Rahul Mangharam,Truong X. Nghiem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应可微模型预测轮廓控制（AD-MPCC），结合在线参数估计与可微MPCC，通过Pacejka魔术公式和递减指数权重滚动时域估计实时适应路面变化，并利用Pacejka-informed机器学习模型在线调整目标权重，在仿真中实现了比基线控制器更快的圈速和更好的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主赛车在不同路面条件下（如干燥、湿滑）的动力学特性变化显著，传统MPCC控制器因固定参数和权重难以适应多变路面，导致性能下降或安全隐患。

Method: 1) 使用参数化Pacejka魔术公式结合带递减指数权重的正则化滚动时域估计进行在线参数估计；2) 提出可微MPCC（Diff-MPCC）框架，通过反向传播优化目标权重；3) 训练Pacejka-informed机器学习模型，以监督学习方式模仿Diff-MPCC的权重调优行为，用于在线部署。

Result: 在单一路面和多种路面切换的仿真场景中，AD-MPCC均比基线控制器实现了更快的圈速，并可靠地保证了行车安全。

Conclusion: AD-MPCC通过在线参数估计与可微权重优化相结合，有效提升了自主赛车在变路面条件下的适应能力和性能，为实际部署提供了可行的框架。

Abstract: This paper presents Adaptive Differentiable Model Predictive Contouring Control (AD-MPCC), a framework for autonomous racing that integrates differentiable MPCC with online parameter estimation to handle varying road-surface conditions. For online parameter estimation, we leverage a parameterized Pacejka Magic Formula together with a regularized moving-horizon estimation scheme with exponentially decaying weights to capture road interactions and update parameters in real time. Furthermore, we propose a differentiable MPCC (Diff-MPCC) framework that enables optimal adjustment of objective weights based on predefined long-horizon performance costs. To implement Diff-MPCC for online objective weight adaptation, we propose a Pacejka-informed machine learning model that is trained in a supervised manner using data generated by Diff-MPCC to tune the objective weights. Simulation results demonstrate that AD-MPCC reliably ensures safety and achieves faster lap times compared to baseline controllers in both single-surface and multiple-surface scenarios.

</details>


### [156] [Stop Pretending Social Robots Are Inevitable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00142)
*Serge Thill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文批判了RO-MAN和HRI会议将人机共融社会描绘为必然趋势的做法，认为这种叙事会误导研究导向：鼓励脱离生态效度的场景设计、忽视实际社会需求，并削弱使机器人成为真正社会智能体的技术研发。作者主张拒绝"不可避免的社交机器人"框架，转而聚焦支撑HRI中"智能"的认知机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对RO-MAN和HRI会议近年来将人机共融社会视为"必然"的叙事倾向感到担忧，认为这种预设会扭曲研究方向和资源分配，导致科研偏离真正有价值的问题。

Method: 通过批判性分析当前HRI领域的研究范式，指出"人机社会必然论"带来的三个问题：1）鼓励无生态效度的场景设计；2）忽视现实社会需求；3）削弱核心技术（尤其是使机器人成为真正社交主体的能力）开发。并提出替代方案：重新聚焦认知机制（即HRI中的"I"）。

Result: 揭示了"必然论"框架对科研的负面影响，包括偏离科学进步、助长技术解决主义、且自相矛盾地阻碍了实现该未来所需的研究。

Conclusion: 应拒绝将HRI领域框定为"不可避免的社交机器人"的叙事，转而采用更开放的框架——无论未来如何都能推动领域进步。具体建议是重新关注支撑人机交互中"智能"的认知机制。

Abstract: This paper takes issue with the recent themes of both the RO-MAN and the HRI conferences for their portrayal of a future human-robot society as inevitable. The focus is on discussing how such statements ultimately shape research. By treating a future human-robot society as a fait accompli, license is given for user studies to imagine any scenario they like, no matter whether it has any ecological relevance, and to emphasise the scenario design over actually creating robot abilities needed to fullfill the imagined role. Meanwhile, research that focusses on actual societal needs, without assuming that robots are a solution, is deprioritised, as is technical development, in particular with respect to abilities that are necessary to enable robots that function as social agents rather than a mere automation of tasks. A frame that simply assumes a robot future not only detracts from scientific advancement in favour of a techno-solutionism we ought to resist, it is also self-defeating as it risks stifling the research needed to bring it about. We should therefore reject attempts to frame and promote the field in terms of the inevitable social robot and instead focus on one that facilitates advances in the field regardless of what the future holds. This paper suggests that a renewed focus on cognitive mechanisms necessary for the "I" in HRI would be a good starting point.

</details>


### [157] [Iterated Invariant EKF for 3D Landmark-Aided Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00145)
*Hilton Marques Souza Santana,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Marco Antonio Meggiolaro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次将迭代不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（IterIEKF）应用于基于地标的惯性3D定位，通过数值仿真证明其在估计精度和一致性上优于SO(3)-EKF、迭代SO(3)-EKF和IEKF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SO(3)-EKF存在虚假可观测性问题，导致滤波器在不可观方向过度自信，估计性能下降。IEKF虽有所改进，但其测量更新不完全满足状态兼容性。因此需要更优的滤波方法。

Method: 将IterIEKF方法首次应用于基于地标的惯性3D定位问题，该方法在低噪声条件下确保估计状态保持在观测状态流形上，同时将不确定性限制在切空间内。

Result: 数值仿真表明，IterIEKF在估计精度和一致性方面均优于SO(3)-EKF、迭代SO(3)-EKF和IEKF。

Conclusion: IterIEKF有效解决了惯性导航定位中的虚假可观测性问题，是一种更优的滤波方案，适用于地标辅助的惯性三维定位。

Abstract: Inertial navigation systems aided by three-dimensional landmark measurements constitute a fundamental problem in robotic perception and state estimation. Classical SO(3)-based Extended Kalman Filter (SO(3)-EKF) approaches provide practical solutions, but suffer from the false observability problem, in which the filter becomes overconfident in unobservable directions, leading to degraded estimation performance. The Invariant EKF (IEKF) addresses this limitation by reformulating the system dynamics as a group-affine system on a Lie group, although its measurement update does not fully satisfy certain state compatibility properties. More recently, the Iterated Invariant EKF (IterIEKF) was proposed to further improve the IEKF by ensuring, in the low-noise regime, that the estimated state remains on the observed state manifold while the uncertainty is confined to its tangent space. In this work, we formulate and apply the IterIEKF to landmark-based inertial 3D localization for the first time. Through numerical simulations, we show that the proposed approach outperforms the classical SO(3)-EKF, the Iterated SO(3)-EKF, and the IEKF in terms of both estimation accuracy and consistency.

</details>


### [158] [3D Point World Models: Point Completion Enables More Accurate Dynamics Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00148)
*Skand Peri,Hung Nguyen,Chanho Kim,Li Fuxin,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出3D Point World Models (3DPWM)，一种完全在3D空间操作的任务无关世界模型，通过先补全部分点云再学习动作条件动力学，实现长程可靠 rollout 和基于模型的规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的动力学模型缺乏3D结构导致几何不一致的长程预测误差，而基于部分点云的3D动力学模型对遮挡和累积漂移敏感。

Method: 先补全部分点云（完成3D场景），再在补全后的3D场景上学习动作条件动力学模型，完全在3D空间操作。

Result: 在不同机器人平台和桌面操作基准测试中，实现100-300+步的长程可靠rollout，支持开环和闭环规划，并成功实现sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: 通过3D场景补全+动作条件动力学建模，3DPWM有效解决了长程预测的几何一致性问题，为任务无关的模型规划提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Learning predictive models of the world enables robotic control through planning, potentially allowing robots to improvise solutions on new tasks. However, large video-based dynamics models lack explicit 3D spatial structure and suffer from geometrically inconsistent long-term rollouts with compounding errors. Emerging 3D dynamics models based on partial point clouds improve geometric consistency but remain sensitive to occlusions and accumulated prediction drift. To address these challenges, we present 3D Point World Models (3DPWM) - a task-agnostic world model that operates entirely in 3D space by first completing partial point clouds and then learning action-conditioned dynamics in this completed 3D scene. By operating on completed geometry, 3DPWM enables reliable long-horizon rollouts and more accurate cost evaluation for model-based planning while supporting adaptation to new tasks. Experiments across different robotic embodiments and tabletop manipulation benchmarks demonstrate that 3DPWM achieves significantly more reliable long-horizon rollouts (100-300+ steps), supports both open-loop and closed-loop planning, and enables successful sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [159] [Dual-Informed Vertical Expansion for Multi-Objective Node Selection in Anytime Conflict-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00156)
*Willem van Osselaer,Jiarui Li,Meshal Alharbi,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DIVE（双知垂直扩展）策略，将CBS算法的节点选择作为一等设计选择，通过最佳边界下潜与深度优先交替的方式，在保证最优性的同时，减少下潜中断、提供早期可行解、降低队列内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CBS的最佳优先节点选择策略虽然节点扩展高效，但存在三个问题：维护庞大frontier、打断父子扩展序列、直到终止才能提供可行解。作者希望将节点选择作为可设计的一等要素来改进。

Method: 提出DIVE策略：每次下潜从当前最佳边界开始，下潜内采用深度优先沿有希望的子节点扩展，利用父子局部性；同时使用incumbent剪枝限制无产出分支。通过分支定界视角形式化证明改变遍历策略不影响最优性。

Result: 在标准MAPF基准测试中，DIVE相比最佳优先搜索：一致减少下潜中断次数；提供带有认证间隙的早期可行解；大幅降低队列内存使用；在密集或内存受限场景下，受益于热启动和简单响应变体。

Conclusion: 最佳优先搜索节点高效，迭代加深内存高效，DIVE下潜高效同时保持定期最佳边界重锚定。三种策略在扩展节点数、下潜中断、队列大小和原对偶边界进展上呈现互补的权衡关系。

Abstract: Conflict-Based Search (CBS) is a leading exact algorithm for Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF), but its high-level node-selection rule is usually treated as a fixed implementation detail. Standard best-first selection is strong for minimizing expanded nodes and closing the optimality certificate, yet it can maintain a large frontier, interrupt parent-child expansion sequences, and provide no feasible incumbent until termination. This paper studies node selection as a first-class design choice for exact CBS. We introduce Dual-Informed Vertical Expansion (DIVE), a policy that is best-bound between dives and depth-oriented within a dive. DIVE starts each dive from the current best-bound frontier, follows promising children to exploit parent-child locality, and uses incumbent pruning to limit unproductive excursions. We formalize CBS node selection through a branch-and-bound view, prove that the traversal policy can be changed without affecting exactness, and analyze the resulting trade-offs among expanded nodes, dive breaks, queue size, and primal-dual bound progress. The analysis predicts three complementary extremes. Best-first search is node efficient, iterative deepening is memory efficient, and DIVE is dive efficient while retaining regular best-bound reanchoring. Experiments on standard MAPF benchmarks support this trade-off map. DIVE consistently reduces dive breaks, provides early incumbents with certified gaps, uses substantially less queue memory than best-first search, and benefits from warm starts and simple responsive variants in dense or memory-limited regimes.

</details>


### [160] [Distributed Multi Robot Lunar Cargo Transportation via Phase Decomposed Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00160)
*Ashutosh Mishra,Elian Neppel,Shreya Santra,Antoine Jonquières,Muhammad Athallah Naufal,Kentaro Uno,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于阶段分解强化学习的模块化可重构机器人协同货物运输框架，在仿真和JAXA实际测试中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化可重构机器人系统在月球任务中面临形态依赖拓扑变化、强负载耦合、长时序决策及安全约束等挑战，现有方法难以高效解决协同货物运输问题。

Method: 将任务分解为抬升、运输、放置三个阶段，每个阶段用专用联合状态策略优化；采用集中训练分散执行范式，结合马尔可夫状态表示的确定性阶段控制器和故障敏感同步机制。

Result: 在仿真环境和JAXA月球测试设施的实际实验中，所有阶段均实现了可靠的协同运输。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了模块化机器人协同运输中的拓扑变化、耦合、长时序决策和安全约束等难题，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Modular reconfigurable robotic systems provide a scalable solution for cooperative surface operations in future lunar missions. However, cooperative cargo transportation remains challenging due to morphology-dependent topology changes, strong payload-induced coupling, long-horizon decision making, and safety constraints. This paper proposes a phase-decomposed reinforcement learning framework for cooperative cargo transport with distributed robotic units. The task is decomposed into lifting, transportation, and placement, each optimized with a dedicated joint-state policy capturing inter-agent coupling. Centralized training promotes stable convergence, while deployment uses onboard proprioception for control and OptiTrack motion capture for ground-truth evaluation and post-processed metrics. A deterministic phase controller expressed in Markov state representation regulates transitions between stages, and a failure-sensitive synchronization mechanism ensures coordinated progression and safety-aware halting during real-world execution. The framework is evaluated in simulation and through controlled field experiments at a JAXA space exploration test facility. Results demonstrate reliable cooperative transport across all stages in both simulation and hardware experiments.

</details>


### [161] [HydraCollab: Adaptive Collaborative-Perception for Distributed Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00191)
*Luke Chen,Cheng-Ju Wu,David R. Martin,Qilin Ye,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HydraCollab，一种自适应协同感知框架，通过选择性传输最信息量的传感器特征并基于空间置信度图动态切换协作策略（中间/晚期），在降低通信开销的同时保持甚至提升感知精度。在多个数据集上相比SOTA方法，带宽使用大幅减少（41%和26%），性能还有小幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协同感知系统面临通信带宽与感知精度之间的固有矛盾：信息交换越多感知效果越好，但通信开销也越大。真实网络存在带宽约束，需要在不牺牲感知性能的前提下最小化通信开销。

Method: HydraCollab框架包含两个核心机制：(i) 选择性传输最具信息量的传感器特征；(ii) 基于空间置信度图动态选择协作策略（中间协作或晚期协作），实现自适应协同感知。

Result: 在V2X-R、V2X-Radar和UAV3D-mini三个数据集上验证，相比SOTA方法Where2comm，HydraCollab仅使用41%（V2X-R）和26%（V2X-Radar）的带宽，同时性能分别提升0.78%和0.75%。

Conclusion: HydraCollab在准确率与通信成本之间实现了最佳整体平衡，为带宽受限的真实场景下的协同感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Collaborative-perception enables multi-robot systems to enhance situational awareness by sharing perceptual information. Existing collaborative-perception systems face an inherent trade-off between communication bandwidth requirements and perception accuracy, where methods that exchange more information achieve better perception results at the cost of increased communication overhead. However, real-world communication networks impose bandwidth constraints that require minimizing communication overhead without sacrificing perception performance. To address this challenge, we propose HydraCollab, an adaptive collaborative-perception framework that (i) selectively transmits the most informative sensor features and (ii) dynamically employs collaboration strategies (intermediate or late) based on spatial confidence maps. Extensive evaluations on the V2X-R, V2X-Radar and UAV3D-mini datasets demonstrate that HydraCollab achieves the best overall trade-off between accuracy and communication cost among existing collaborative-perception methods. Relative to SOTA Where2comm, HydraCollab uses only 41% of the bandwidth on V2X-R and 26% on V2X-Radar while improving performance by 0.78% and 0.75% respectively. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/AICPS/HydraCollab.

</details>


### [162] [ELMP: Efficient Learning for Motion Planning via Analytical Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00215)
*Yixiao Li,Tifanny Portela,Jordis Herrmann,René Zurbrügg,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ELMP框架，通过自监督微调实现神经运动规划器的数据高效适应，无需重新收集大量专家数据，在成功率、冷启动延迟等方面显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经运动规划器虽能快速生成运动，但适应新环境通常需要重新收集大量专家数据，计算成本过高。现有方法缺乏数据高效的适应机制。

Method: 提出ELMP：1) 通过可微运动学层，使用密集碰撞、目标到达和平滑性目标直接优化策略，替代专家数据生成；2) 引入点云显式编码工具几何结构，支持不同运动链的鲁棒泛化。

Result: 平均成功率84.8%，冷启动延迟比经典方法低数个数量级；自监督微调将零样本的57.3%提升至89.8%；推理延迟保持在毫秒级。

Conclusion: ELMP有效消除了数据收集瓶颈，实现了数据高效的神经运动规划器适应，并在真实机器人上得到验证。

Abstract: Neural Motion Planners (NMPs) enable fast reactive motion generation, but adapting them to new environments typically requires recollecting large expert datasets, which is computationally prohibitive. We propose ELMP, a framework for data-efficient adaptation via self-supervised fine-tuning. Rather than generating additional expert trajectories with expensive global planners, ELMP directly optimizes the policy through a differentiable kinematic layer using dense collision, target-reaching, and smoothness objectives. This replaces expert data generation with rapid problem sampling, reducing per-sample adaptation cost by roughly two orders of magnitude. To further support robust generalization across changing kinematic chains, we introduce a mechanism to explicitly encode tool geometry via point clouds. Benchmarked against classical and neural baselines, ELMP achieves an 84.8% average success rate with orders-of-magnitude lower cold-start latency than classical methods. In unseen environments, self-supervised fine-tuning improves success rate from 57.3% (zero-shot) to 89.8%, removing the data collection bottleneck. Our approach maintains millisecond-level inference latency and is validated on a physical Franka Emika Panda robot.

</details>


### [163] [ASPIRE: Agentic /Skills Discovery for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00272)
*Runyu Lu,Yubo Wu,Ethan Kou,Letian Fu,Wenli Xiao,Ajay Mandlekar,Yinzhen Xu,Guanya Shi,Ken Goldberg,Ang Chen,Mosharaf Chowdhury,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Guanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ASPIRE系统，通过持续学习自动编写和优化机器人控制程序，将经验积累为可复用的技能库，在多种任务和场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人编程需要协调多模态感知、管理物理接触动力学、处理多样化配置和执行失败，挑战巨大，亟需自动化编程方法。

Method: ASPIRE包含三个组件：(1)闭环执行引擎，提供细粒度多模态轨迹以支持自主故障诊断、修复合成与验证；(2)持续扩展的技能库，将验证后的修复蒸馏为可重用知识；(3)进化搜索，生成多样化任务序列和控制程序以超越单轨迹优化。

Result: 在LIBERO-Pro扰动下提升77%，Robosuite双手递物提升72%，BEHAVIOR-1K长时家务提升32%；在零样本泛化中ASPIRE达到31%成功率，对比基线仅4%。

Conclusion: ASPIRE通过自主迭代探索和技能库积累，实现了跨任务、跨仿真/真实场景、跨本体的有效编程，初步证据显示仿真技能可迁移到真实机器人。

Abstract: Traditional robot programming is challenging: it requires orchestrating multimodal perception, managing physical contact dynamics, and handling diverse configurations and execution failures. We introduce ASPIRE (Agentic Skill Programming through Iterative Robot Exploration), a continual learning system that autonomously writes and refines robot control programs in a code-as-policy paradigm while compounding experience into a reusable skill library. ASPIRE discovers skills that persist across tasks, simulation and real-world settings, and embodiments. It operates in an open-ended loop with three components: (1) a closed-loop robot execution engine that exposes fine-grained multimodal traces, enabling autonomous failure diagnosis, repair synthesis, and validation; (2) a continually expanding skill library that distills validated fixes into reusable, transferable knowledge; and (3) evolutionary search that generates diverse task sequences and control programs to explore beyond single-trajectory refinement. ASPIRE surpasses prior methods by up to 77% on LIBERO-Pro manipulation under perturbation, 72% on Robosuite bimanual handover, and 32% on BEHAVIOR-1K long-horizon household tasks. Its accumulated library also enables zero-shot generalization to unseen long-horizon tasks: on LIBERO-Pro Long, ASPIRE achieves 31% success versus 4% for prior methods despite their use of test-time reasoning and retries. Finally, simulation-discovered skills provide initial evidence of sim-to-real transfer, substantially reducing real-robot programming effort across different embodiments and robot APIs.

</details>


### [164] [What's Hidden Matters: Identifying Planning-Critical Occluded Agents using Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00283)
*Amirhosein Chahe,Tyler Naes,Jovin D'sa,Faizan M. Tariq,Sangjae Bae,Lifeng Zhou,David Isele*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于规划KL散度（PKL）的框架，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）识别并推理对自车轨迹最关键的遮挡区域，通过PKL引导的数据选择策略训练模型，使较小模型在零样本场景下超越大模型，性能提升约30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么对所有遮挡区域采用统一的保守策略，导致过度防御；要么推断遮挡空间却不评估其对规划器的实际影响。感知与规划之间存在关键鸿沟。

Method: 提出PKL（规划KL散度）作为信息论度量，系统识别并排序对自车规划影响最大的遮挡区域；利用GPT-5生成结构化标注数据；基于nuScenes数据集构建基准；通过PKL引导的数据选择策略微调各类VLM。

Result: 微调后的小模型显著优于零样本大模型；PKL引导的数据选择策略相较于随机采样提升约30%性能；所有通用及领域适应VLM在微调后均有大幅性能提升。

Conclusion: 首次系统性地训练VLM关注规划关键的遮挡区域，实现了更语义化、高效的自动驾驶风险评估。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must safely navigate complex environments where planning-critical agents may be hidden from view. Current approaches often treat all occlusions with uniform conservatism, yielding needlessly defensive driving, or they infer hidden spaces without estimating the impact on the planner. This work bridges the critical gap between perception and planning by enabling Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to identify and reason about the specific hidden agents that are most critical to the ego-vehicle's trajectory. We introduce a novel framework that uses Planning KL-divergence (PKL), an information-theoretic metric, to systematically identify and rank occluded agents based on their impact on the ego vehicle's plan. Using this planning-aware ranking, we employ an expert VLM (GPT-5) to generate rich, structured annotations that capture the visual evidence and reasoning required for this task. We apply this framework to the nuScenes dataset to create a new benchmark focused on high-impact scenarios. We conduct comprehensive experiments on a wide range of general-purpose and domain-adapted VLMs, demonstrating that fine-tuning on our PKL-guided data yields dramatic performance improvements across all models. Notably, our results show that smaller, fine-tuned models significantly outperform their much larger zero-shot counterparts, and that our PKL-guided data selection strategy improves performance by approximately 30\% over random sampling. Our work presents the first systematic approach for training VLMs to focus on planning-critical occlusions, enabling more semantically grounded and efficient risk assessment in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [165] [NeHMO: Neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability Learning for Decentralized Safe Multi-Arm Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00326)
*Qingyi Chen,Zachary Kingston,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经汉密尔顿-雅可比可达性（HJR）学习的方法，用于多机械臂运动规划中的安全约束。该方法通过学习最坏情况下的安全值函数，结合去中心化轨迹优化，实现了可扩展、数据高效且泛化性强的实时规划，在复杂多臂任务中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式多臂规划方法可协调所有机械臂但可扩展性差，难以满足实时应用；去中心化方法虽具可扩展性，但依赖准确的行为预测或协调协议，当其他机械臂行为不可预测时容易失效。因此需要一种既能处理最坏情况安全约束，又具备可扩展性和实时性的方法。

Method: 提出神经HJR学习方法，近似一个能够捕捉机械臂间最坏情况安全约束的安全值函数；基于该学习到的HJR表示，进一步开发去中心化轨迹优化框架，实现实时规划。

Result: 该方法具有可扩展性和数据效率，能够泛化到多机械臂系统，在具有挑战性的多臂运动规划任务上优于当前最优基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将HJR学习与去中心化规划结合，在保证安全性的同时突破了集中式方法的可扩展性瓶颈，为多机械臂实时安全运动规划提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: Safe multi-arm motion planning is a challenging problem in robotics due to its high dimensionality, coupled configuration space, and complex collision constraints. Centralized planners are capable of coordinating all arms but often face scalability limitations, restricting applicability in real-time settings. On the other hand, decentralized methods are scalable and recent deep learning-based approaches have shown promising results. However, these depend on accurate behavior prediction or coordination protocols and may fail when other arms act unpredictably. To address these challenges, we introduce a neural Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability (HJR) learning-based approach to approximate a safety value function that captures worst-case inter-arm safety constraints. We further develop a decentralized trajectory optimization framework that uses the learned HJR representation for real-time planning. The proposed method is scalable and data-efficient, generalizes across multi-manipulator systems, and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on challenging multi-arm motion planning tasks.

</details>


### [166] [Unleashing More Actions via Action Compositional Training for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00351)
*Kai Peng,Jie Lu,Xiaojiang Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型在机器人操作中因数据过拟合导致无法泛化到新子技能组合的问题，提出ACT-VLA框架，通过离线数据增强利用模型潜在任务表示自动合成新演示，无需额外人工采集数据，显著提升模型泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在标准训练范式下严重过拟合于特定行为模式，即使只需重组已有子技能也无法泛化到分布外场景；而扩展数据集成本高昂，因此需要一种无需人工遥操作即可自动扩大训练分布的方法。

Method: 提出ACT-VLA框架，一种离线数据增强方法。利用模型自身的潜在任务表示，从现有任务中直接合成新颖且物理有效的演示数据，用于策略训练，自动扩展训练分布并缓解过拟合。

Result: 在模拟环境的挑战性操作任务中，基线VLA模型因原始分布过拟合泛化能力差，而使用ACT-VLA合成数据训练的策略成功率显著更高。

Conclusion: 利用现有任务进行自动化演示合成为一种有效、可扩展且数据高效的方法，能够拓宽VLA模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models excel at robotic manipulation, driven by the scale and diversity of demonstration data. However, standard training paradigms often cause VLA models to severely overfit to specific behavioral patterns, rendering them unable to generalize to out-of-distribution scenarios even when those scenarios merely require novel combinations of identical sub-skills. While expanding datasets can mitigate this overfitting, acquiring high-quality robot data remains notoriously labor-intensive and cost-prohibitive. To resolve this impasse without expensive human teleoperation and to truly unleash more actions,i.e., enable VLA models to compose known sub-skills into a much broader set of executable behaviors beyond the original demonstrations-we propose ACT-VLA (Action Compositional Training for VLA Models), an offline data augmentation framework that leverages the model's latent task representations to synthesize novel, physically valid demonstrations directly from existing tasks for policy training. By eliminating additional manual data collection, our method automatically expands the training distribution and mitigates overfitting. We evaluate our approach on challenging manipulation tasks in simulation. Experiments demonstrate that while baseline VLA models generalize poorly due to original distribution overfitting, policies trained with our synthesized data achieve substantially higher success rates, validating that leveraging existing tasks for automated demonstration synthesis provides an effective, scalable, and data-efficient route to broadening VLA generalization.

</details>


### [167] [Robust Operational Space Control with Conformal Disturbance Bounds for Safe Redundant Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00424)
*Wenhua Liu,Fan Zhang,Qin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对冗余机械臂在约束与人机交互环境下的任务空间跟踪与安全保障问题，提出将扩展状态观测器（ESO）与保形预测（Conformal Prediction）相结合的鲁棒操作空间计算力矩控制框架。ESO直接估计操作空间中的集总扰动，无需完整状态测量；鲁棒控制屏障函数（CBF）保障安全；滑动窗口保形预测在线估计扰动变化界，实现实用概率安全保证。7自由度Franka机械臂实验验证了毫米级跟踪精度与1kHz实时安全控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典操作空间计算力矩控制器（OSCTC）依赖精确动力学模型，在扰动存在时性能退化；而残差学习等数据驱动方法需要完整状态测量（噪声大、缺乏理论保证、设计复杂）。亟需一种兼具模型鲁棒性与数据驱动适应性的控制框架。

Method: 1）扩展状态观测器（ESO）直接估计操作空间中的集总扰动，无需完整状态测量；2）基于ESO构建鲁棒控制屏障函数（CBF）来保障不确定性下的安全性；3）采用滑动窗口保形预测机制在线估计扰动变化界，实现分布无关的实用概率安全保证。

Result: 在7自由度Franka Research 3机械臂上，在多种扰动条件下实现了毫米级跟踪精度，并在1kHz频率下实现了实时安全控制。

Conclusion: 所提出的ESO+保形预测框架成功融合了模型基鲁棒性与数据驱动适应性，解决了经典OSCTC依赖精确模型和纯数据驱动方法缺乏理论保证的双重问题，为冗余机械臂在约束/人机交互环境下的高精度、安全控制提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Redundant robotic manipulators operating in constrained and human-interactive environments require accurate task-space tracking together with rigorous safety guarantees under dynamic uncertainties. Classical operational space computed torque controller (OSCTC) relies on accurate dynamic models and degrades in the presence of disturbances. In contrast, the data-driven paradigm of residual learning approximates disturbances as functions learned from full-state measurements, which are often noisy in practice, lack rigorous theoretical guarantees, and introduce additional design complexity. This paper proposes a robust OSCTC framework that integrates an extended state observer (ESO) with conformal prediction to combine model-based robustness and data-driven adaptability. The ESO estimates lumped disturbances directly in operational space without requiring full-state measurements as in residual learning, and a robust control barrier function (CBF) is constructed to enforce safety under uncertainty. However, robust CBFs require a known disturbance-variation bound to guarantee absolute safety, which often leads to conservatism in practice. To address this limitation, we further employ a sliding-window conformal prediction mechanism to estimate the bound online in a distribution-free manner, thereby achieving practical probabilistic safety guarantees. Experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Research 3 manipulator demonstrate millimeter-level tracking accuracy and real-time safe control at 1~kHz under various disturbances.

</details>


### [168] [Learning Gait-Aware Quadruped Locomotion with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00442)
*Merve Atasever,Cagan Bakirci,Alfredo Reina Corona,Keyan Azbijari,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用信号时序逻辑（STL）约束来生成奖励函数，用于四足机器人步态控制，替代传统手工设计奖励，在Barkour机器人上实现了更优的速度跟踪和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习四足步态依赖固定、手工设计的马尔可夫奖励函数，缺乏可解释性且无法显式控制步态行为。

Method: 使用参数化STL约束（安全边界、步态同步、指令跟踪等）定义三种步态模板，通过平滑鲁棒性近似计算稠密奖励，结合PPO算法在MuJoCo XLA中并行训练并引入域随机化。

Result: 相比手工奖励基线，STL奖励实现了更紧的速度跟踪和更稳定的训练过程。

Conclusion: STL形式的约束能有效编码步态行为，生成的可塑奖励在四足机器人控制中优于传统手工奖励设计。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for quadruped locomotion commonly depends on fixed, hand-crafted, and Markovian reward functions that limit both interpretability of learned policies and lack explicit control over gait behaviors. We introduce a framework where distinct gaits are specified using parameterized constraints expressed in Signal Temporal Logic (STL). These include safety bounds, gait synchronization constraints, command tracking, and actuation bounds. From these specifications, we develop a reward shaping mechanism that provides learning agents a dense, continuous reward landscape that encodes desired behavior. We define parametric STL templates for three speed regimes (walking-trot, trot, bound), calibrate their parameters from reference rollouts, and compute rewards from using smooth approximations of STL robustness over the rollouts. The generated rewards can be used to provide shaped gradients compatible with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO). We instantiate the approach on Google's Barkour quadruped robot in MuJoCo XLA (MJX). We use parallelization within the simulator to improve training speeds and use domain randomization to robustify learned policies. We show that compared to a baseline of hand-crafted rewards, the STL-shaped rewards yield tighter velocity tracking and more stable training. Videos can be found on our project website: https://stl-locomotion.github.io/.

</details>


### [169] [Search-Based Spatiotemporal and Multi-Robot Motion Planning on Graphs of Space-Time Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00444)
*Jingtao Tang,Zining Mao,Lufan Yang,Hang Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于时空凸集图（ST-GCS）的算法框架，将碰撞自由区域表示为时空中的凸集，通过图搜索和连续轨迹优化实现单/多机器人时间最优运动规划，实验表明在狭窄瞬态可行区域中显著加速，可扩展至100台机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人运动规划中，碰撞自由区域随时间变化且几何约束复杂，在连续空间中处理瞬态可行区域具有挑战性，现有方法效率不足。

Method: 1) 构建时空凸集图（ST-GCS）表示碰撞自由区域；2) 将时间最优规划转化为图搜索问题，结合最佳优先搜索与连续轨迹优化；3) 提出精确凸分解（ECD）方案处理动态障碍物；4) 结合优先级规划与窗口协调策略实现多机器人运动规划。

Result: 单/多机器人实验中显著快于多种规划器，保持高质量解；大规模演示中可在数分钟内求解多达100台机器人的实例。

Conclusion: ST-GCS框架有效解决了狭窄瞬态可行区域中的运动规划难题，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Spatiotemporal motion planning, especially in multi-robot settings, requires robots to reason about collision-free regions that change over time, which is challenging in continuous spaces when feasible regions are transient and geometrically constrained. We present an algorithmic framework based on graphs of space-time convex sets (ST-GCSs), where collision-free regions are represented as convex sets in space-time and trajectories correspond to paths on the graph together with continuous motions within the selected sets. We formulate time-optimal planning on ST-GCSs as a graph-search problem over path-indexed states and develop a best-first search solver that evaluates partial paths via continuous trajectory optimization, guided by admissible heuristics and dominance checks. We further present an Exact Convex Decomposition (ECD) scheme to reserve trajectory occupancies in space-time, enabling unified handling of dynamic obstacles and multi-robot interactions. For multi-robot motion planning, we integrate ST-GCS planning and ECD into prioritized planning methods and introduce a windowed coordination scheme to improve efficiency. Extensive experiments on single-robot and multi-robot problems demonstrate substantial speedups over various planners while maintaining high solution quality, particularly in environments with narrow and transient feasible regions. Large-scale demonstrations further show that the proposed multi-robot motion planner can solve instances with up to $100$ robots within only a few minutes. Project homepage: https://sites.google.com/view/stgcs

</details>


### [170] [VLM-AR3L: Vision-Language Models for Absolute and Relative Rewards in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00483)
*Kuan-Chen Chen,Winston Chen,Wei-Fang Sun,Min-Chun Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-AR3L框架，利用视觉语言模型(VLM)为强化学习提供绝对奖励和相对奖励，在多个基准任务上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中，尤其是在开放式环境里，设计有效的奖励函数非常困难，因为任务目标往往是抽象的且难以量化。

Method: 利用VLM解释智能体的视觉观察与自然语言任务目标之间的关系，从VLM生成的偏好标签中学习绝对奖励模型（评估单状态）和相对奖励模型（比较连续观察以推断进展），并将二者整合。

Result: 在经典控制、操作和开放世界具身任务基准上，特别是在Minecraft中，VLM-AR3L持续优于先前的基于VLM的奖励学习方法。

Conclusion: 结合状态评估的稳定性与比较监督的鲁棒性，VLM-AR3L有效解决了开放式环境中奖励设计的难题，是一种更优的VLM驱动奖励学习方案。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions remains a major challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), particularly in open-ended environments where task goals are abstract and difficult to quantify. In this work, we present VLM-AR3L, a framework that leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to provide both absolute and relative rewards for RL. VLM-AR3L interprets an agent's visual observations in the context of a natural language task goal, and learns both absolute and relative rewards from VLM-generated preference labels. The absolute reward model predicts scalar evaluations for individual states, while the relative reward model compares consecutive observations to infer progress or regression toward the task goal. Their integration combines the stability of state-based evaluation with the robustness of comparative supervision. We evaluate VLM-AR3L across benchmarks spanning classic control, manipulation, and open-world embodied tasks, with a particular focus on Minecraft given its visual complexity and long-horizon decision-making requirements. Experimental results show that VLM-AR3L consistently outperforms prior VLM-based reward learning methods.

</details>


### [171] [From Technical Metrics to User Perception: A User Study of a Multimodal Human-Robot Interaction System for Object Detection and Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00530)
*Jian Song,Tian Zi,Shen Guanting*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过24人用户实验发现，将机器人抓取系统的任务成功率从75%提升到90%（改进感知与语言模块），用户能显著感知到速度、可靠性和能力的提升，70.83%的用户偏好改进系统，且效应量很大（p<0.001）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术性能的提升（如任务成功率）不一定能被用户在实际交互中感知，现有研究多聚焦基准评测而缺乏用户中心验证，因此需要探究多大程度的性能改进才能转化为用户可感知的差异。

Method: 采用受试者内设计（24人），比较基线系统（Whisper+Florence-2+LLaMA 3.1+Type-2模糊逻辑控制器）与改进系统（替换为Grounding DINO+SAM+Qwen 3.5 9B，控制器不变）在桌面抓取任务上的用户感知差异，使用7点李克特量表评估速度、可靠性、能力和流畅性。

Result: 17/24用户偏好改进系统（p=0.043, h=0.43）；三个感知维度均显著高于基线（Holm校正后p<0.001），效应量为大至非常大。

Conclusion: 15个百分点的成功率提升足以让用户产生可感知的差异，验证了技术改进在交互中的有效性，强调在机器人操控流水线评估中应补充用户中心证据。

Abstract: Improvements in the technical performance of human--robot interaction (HRI) systems do not automatically translate into differences that human users can detect during live interaction. This paper investigates whether a 15 percentage point gain in end-to-end task success (from 75% in a multimodal baseline system to 90% in an improved configuration identified through a prior ablation study) is sufficient to produce consistent and measurable differences in user perception. The baseline system combines Whisper for speech recognition, Florence-2 for open-vocabulary object detection, LLaMA 3.1 for action extraction, and an interval Type-2 fuzzy logic controller for motion execution. The improved configuration replaces the perception and language modules with Grounding DINO + SAM and Qwen 3.5 9B, respectively, while retaining the same controller. A within-subject user study with 24 participants compared both systems on the same tabletop object-grasping task. After interacting with each configuration, participants rated perceived speed, reliability, and overall competence and fluency on a 7-point Likert scale. Results show that 17 out of 24 participants (70.83%) preferred the improved system (exact binomial test, p = 0.043, h = 0.43), and all three perceptual constructs were rated significantly higher for the improved configuration after Holm correction, with large to very large effect sizes (p < 0.001). These findings confirm that the identified technical improvements are perceptible to users in direct interaction and underscore the importance of complementing benchmark evaluation with user-centred evidence when assessing robotic manipulation pipelines.

</details>


### [172] [Learning from Demonstration via Spatiotemporal Tubes for Unknown Euler-Lagrange Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00534)
*Ratnangshu Das,Puneeth Shankar,Varuni Buereddy,Ravi Prakash,Pushpak Jagtap*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STT-LfD框架，通过异方差高斯过程学习时空管（STT）作为意图包络，并设计闭环反馈控制器，在不需显式系统辨识的情况下实现未知欧拉-拉格朗日系统的模仿学习，在移动机器人和7自由度机械臂上验证了鲁棒性和计算效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LfD方法将运动学习与控制解耦，仅跟踪固定参考轨迹，无法处理未知动力学系统且缺乏对演示中时变精度要求的建模。

Method: 1) 利用异方差高斯过程从演示中学习时空管（STT）作为数据驱动的安全规范；2) 设计闭环反馈控制器在满足执行器约束的前提下强制执行学习的STT约束；3) 避免显式系统辨识。

Result: 在移动机器人和7自由度机械臂硬件实验中，优于基线方法在抗扰动鲁棒性和计算速度方面的表现。

Conclusion: STT-LfD通过将演示视为安全规范而非固定参考，保留了任务的时序结构，计算高效，且无需系统辨识即可实现鲁棒控制。

Abstract: We present STT-LfD, a unified Learning from Demonstration (LfD) framework that integrates motion learning with control for unknown Euler-Lagrange systems. Unlike traditional decoupled approaches that track a fixed reference, the proposed method treats demonstrations as a data-driven safety specification. Using heteroscedastic Gaussian Processes, STT-LfD learns Spatiotemporal Tubes (STTs) as an intent envelope that capture time-varying precision requirements of a task. A closed-form feedback controller then enforces these learned constraints while respecting actuator limits, without requiring explicit system identification. The approach preserves the temporal structure of demonstrations, remains computationally efficient, and avoids explicit system identification. Hardware experiments on a mobile robot and a 7-DOF manipulator show that it outperforms baselines in robustness to disturbances and computational speed.

</details>


### [173] [[Preprint] Dynamic Modeling, Gait Synthesis, and Control of a Novel Subsurface Bore Propagator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00569)
*Lina van Brügge,Shruti Kotpalliwar,Anton Koval,Akshit Saradagi,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无人类参与的地下勘探和挖掘的模块化新型地下机器人，其设计结合了蚯蚓式锚定推进和隧道掘进机式挖掘。通过欧拉-拉格朗日框架建立动力学模型，设计反馈控制器和步态合成策略，并在Unity仿真和ROS集成中验证，最终实现3个步态周期后总推进30毫米。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现无需人类参与的地下勘探与挖掘，需要一种能够在地下环境中自主推进和掘进的机器人系统。

Method: 1) 将机器人设计为五个模块：钻头模块、两个锚定模块和两个推进模块；2) 利用欧拉-拉格朗日框架建立动力学模型；3) 设计反馈控制器实现各关节的解耦控制；4) 通过集中式状态机进行步态合成；5) 在基于Unity的物理仿真和ROS集成中验证性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，该设计、控制器和步态合成策略能够成功将机器人锚定在原地，并在完成3个步态周期后实现总计30毫米的土壤前进推进。

Conclusion: 所提出的模块化地下机器人设计结合了仿生推进与工程掘进思路，通过建模与控制实现了有效的地下推进，为未来全自主地下勘探机器人奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this article, we present dynamic modeling, gait synthesis, and feedback control design for a modular novel subsurface robot, designed for human-free subsurface exploration and excavation. The subsurface propagator design is based on two major aspects: 1) anchor and propel movement like an earthworm and 2) excavation similar to tunnel boring machines. This design is decoupled into five separate modules: one drill head to excavate and create cavity for propagation, two modules to anchor the robot, and two modules to enable propagation of the body. In order to design a controller for each of the modules, dynamic models using the Euler-Lagrange framework are developed. These mathematical models are used as a baseline to design controlled decoupled operation of the different joint movements. The operation of robotic assembly is constructed via a centralized state machine for gait synthesis with integration of the designed feedback controller. The controllers are tested on the real robot geometry to aid sim-to-real integration: A physics-based Unity simulation using a CAD model of the robot and integration of the trained controller via ROS verifies the performance of the robot. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed design, controllers and the gait synthesis strategy together are capable of anchoring the robot in place and creating an total advancement of 30\,mm into the soil after completing 3 gait cycles.

</details>


### [174] [Enhancing Robustness in Robot-Environment Interactions through Passive Compliant Degrees of Freedom: A Hybrid Position-Force Control Approach with Feedback Linearization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00571)
*Rahman Ardakanian,Iman Kardan,AliAkbar Akbari,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合反馈线性化混合位置-力控制与末端被动柔顺自由度（弹簧-阻尼器）的交互架构，通过在接触点物理耗散冲击能量，减少传统主动控制中接触振荡、力/速度误差和关节扭矩波动。在2-DOF平面机械臂上的仿真验证表明，弹簧-阻尼配置相比刚性或仅弹簧配置，能显著降低力/速度误差标准差（最多降低41.1%）并抑制振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态或非结构化环境中，机器人-环境交互常受冲击、振动以及接触几何与力学特性不确定性的影响，传统主动混合位置-力控制依赖反馈、力传感与增益调节，难以有效抑制高频冲击传播。

Method: 将反馈线性化混合位置-力控制与末端被动柔顺自由度（物理弹簧-阻尼器）相结合，在接触点处物理存储与耗散冲击能量，防止高频冲击传播至驱动关节与力控制回路。

Result: 在2-DOF平面机械臂上对比刚性、仅弹簧、弹簧-阻尼三种末端配置，弹簧-阻尼配置在固定与时变交互条件下均表现出最优性能：固定环境切向力误差标准差降低36.5%，变环境法向力误差标准差降低25.4%，变环境法向速度误差标准差降低41.1%。

Conclusion: 在混合位置-力控制中引入末端被动弹簧-阻尼器能有效衰减接触振荡、降低力/速度误差方差、平滑关节扭矩响应，为动态/非结构化环境下的机器人交互提供一种简单有效的鲁棒性改进方案。

Abstract: Robot-environment interactions in dynamic or unstructured settings are often degraded by impact shocks, vibrations, and uncertainties in contact geometry and mechanical properties. This paper proposes an interaction architecture that combines feedback-linearized hybrid position-force control with a passive compliant degree of freedom embedded at the end-effector. Unlike conventional hybrid position-force control, which relies mainly on active feedback, force sensing, and gain tuning, the proposed architecture uses a physical spring-damper interface to store and dissipate impact energy at the contact point before high-frequency shocks propagate to the actuated joints and force-control loop. The approach is evaluated in MATLAB/Simulink on a 2-DOF planar manipulator with three end-effector configurations: rigid, spring-only, and spring-damper. Results under fixed and time-varying interaction conditions show that the spring-damper configuration provides stronger attenuation of contact-induced oscillations, lower force and velocity error variance, and smoother joint-torque response. Representative reductions include 36.5% in fixed-environment tangential force-error standard deviation, 25.4% in variable-environment normal force-error standard deviation, and 41.1% in variable-environment normal velocity-error standard deviation.

</details>


### [175] [From Real-Time Planning to Reliable Execution:Scalable Coordination for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Fleets in Industrial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00591)
*Bo Cao,Zhe Liu,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCALE是一个反应式在线协调框架，通过运动诱导冲突减少机制和广义共轭动作-优先超图（CAPH），解决异构机器人车队在工业环境中的实时路径规划和鲁棒执行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构机器人车队在工业环境中面临高密度部署和异质运动能力带来的协调挑战，通信延迟、执行不确定性等扰动会导致机器人偏离计划路径，引发等待和拥堵传播。

Method: 提出SCALE反应式在线协调框架，包含两个核心组件：1）运动诱导冲突减少机制，用于在线生成可行路径；2）广义共轭动作-优先超图（CAPH），自适应调整机器人间的优先关系以减轻扰动影响。

Result: 通过大量验证实验和为期三天的仓库部署验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SCALE框架能够实现异构机器人车队的实时在线协调，有效应对扰动，保证系统鲁棒执行。

Abstract: With the increasing deployment of heterogeneous robot fleets in industrial environments, efficient coordination remains a critical challenge. Real-time path planning must simultaneously accommodate high robot densities and heterogeneous motion capabilities, while communication delays, execution uncertainties, and other disturbances may cause robots to deviate from the temporal assumptions underlying planned paths. Such deviations can lead to excessive waiting and congestion propagation across the fleet. This paper presents SCALE, a reactive online coordination framework that enables real-time planning while maintaining robust execution. Within this framework, we introduce a motion-induced conflict reduction mechanism to support the online generation of feasible paths for online conflict resolution. To mitigate the effects of disturbances, we further design a generalized Conjugate Action-Precedence Hypergraph (CAPH) that adaptively adjusts precedence relations among robots. Extensive validation experiments, together with a three-day deployment in a warehouse, demonstrate the

</details>


### [176] [Domain Arithmetic: One-Shot VLA Adaptation under Environmental Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00666)
*Taewook Kang,Taeheon Kim,Donghyun Shin,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DART方法，通过权重向量算术和子空间对齐，仅需单条演示即可让VLA模型适应环境变化（如视角改变、机器人更换），在模拟和真实实验中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在环境变化（如相机位姿改变、机器人型号不同）下性能下降，传统适应方法需要大量演示数据，成本高昂。

Method: 基于类比的方法，通过权重向量算术添加领域特定信息（Domain ARiThmetic），利用子空间对齐对奇异分量进行过滤，仅需单条演示即可实现高效适应。

Result: 在模拟和真实实验中，DART在单次示教场景下，面对多种视觉和机器人本体变化，表现优于现有的VLA适应方法。

Conclusion: DART通过单条演示即可高效适应环境变化，显著降低数据采集和训练成本，为VLA模型的泛化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often fail to perform the same learned tasks under environmental shifts, such as changes in camera pose and shifts to a different but similar robot (e.g., from Panda to UR5e). Adapting these models to the shifted environment (i.e., target domain) often requires training on multiple demonstrations for each task, which are costly to collect. To reduce the burden of data curation and training, we propose an analogy-based method that adapts VLA models under environmental shifts through weight vector arithmetic with domain-specific information addition, named Domain ARiThmetic (DART). Unlike prior approaches, DART requires collecting only a single demonstration, enabling efficient adaptation. To accurately isolate domain-specific information for addition, DART performs subspace alignment between singular components in weight vectors to filter out noisy components. In both simulated and real-world experiments, DART outperforms existing VLA adaptation methods in one-shot scenarios across diverse visual and embodiment shifts. Code is available at https://github.com/snumprlab/dart.

</details>


### [177] [Path Planning in Physically Viable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00673)
*Su Ann Low,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Xingjian Li,Adam J. Thorpe,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理可行世界模型，通过将3D高斯泼溅场景重建与物理仿真结合，使机器人能够在部署前评估未来地形变化（如洪水）对导航路径可行性的影响，无需重新采集传感器数据或重建地图。实验表明，该方法能够暴露仅基于原始地图规划时无法发现的长期路径故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化户外环境中依赖部署前采集的静态地图进行长时域规划，但环境可能发生物理变化（如洪水、滑坡），使原本可行的路径变得危险或不可达。现有方法无法在无需重新建图的情况下评估未来地形变化对路径可行性的影响。

Method: 1) 将3D高斯泼溅场景重建与物理仿真结合，生成地形的物理修改版本；2) 实现地形感知规划器，考虑世界模型模拟的物理事件、障碍物和形变；3) 允许机器人和操作者在执行前评估计划路线的可行性。

Result: 在德克萨斯州中部的真实户外场地，通过模拟不同严重程度的洪水进行评估。结果表明，物理可行世界模型能够暴露长期路径故障和重新路由行为，这些在仅基于原始重建环境规划时无法显现。

Conclusion: 物理可行世界模型使机器人能够在部署前评估未来地形变化对路径可行性的影响，有助于避免在条件变化后无法撤退或恢复的受限环境中的风险。

Abstract: Robots deployed in unstructured outdoor environments often plan from scene reconstructions collected before deployment because operators cannot remap large or remote sites before every mission. As a result, robots must make long-horizon planning decisions using stale maps that assume the terrain remains unchanged, even though physical changes to the environment may render previously feasible routes unsafe or unreachable at execution time. We present a physically viable world model for evaluating what-if queries for robot navigation under future terrain change. The system augments reconstructed 3D Gaussian splat scenes with physics-based simulation to generate physically modified versions of the same environment without recollecting sensor data or rebuilding the map. We then implement a terrain-aware planner that accounts for physical events, obstacles, and deformations that are simulated by the world model. This allows robots and human operators to evaluate whether planned routes remain feasible before committing to a planned route, particularly in constrained environments where retreat or recovery may become impossible once conditions change. We evaluate the system on a real outdoor field site in Central Texas using simulated flooding across multiple severity levels. We measure route and mission feasibility as terrain conditions deteriorate under physically simulated interventions. Our results show that physically viable world models expose long-horizon route failures and rerouting behavior that are not apparent when planning only on the original reconstructed environment, allowing robots to evaluate how future terrain changes may affect route feasibility before deployment.

</details>


### [178] [From Prediction Uncertainty to Conformalized Distance Fields for Safe Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00776)
*Jaeuk Shin,Yoonseok Ra,Insoon Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出功能共形预测（FCP）框架，直接对预测距离场进行共形化，得到分布无关的场级下界，确保任何满足该约束的轨迹都是安全的。通过经验低秩和时不变性实现系数空间分解，离线拟合包络+在线轻量更新，嵌入MPC控制器中。在行人基准和密集3D四旋翼任务中，FCP-MPC在安全、可行性和效率间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形方法对每个障碍物预测误差聚合为一个标量进行共形化，丧失了空间连贯性，且随场景密度增加扩展性差，无法在动态环境中高效进行安全运动规划。

Method: 提出功能共形预测（FCP）框架，直接对整个预测距离场进行共形化，利用残差距离场的经验低秩性和近似时不变性，在系数空间进行分解。离线通过功能主成分分析和高斯混合归纳共形过程拟合包络，在线通过自适应功能共形更新（AFCP）在低维向量上轻量优化。

Result: 在ETH-UCY行人基准和密集3D四旋翼任务（多达280个动态障碍物）中，FCP-MPC在安全、可行性和效率间取得良好平衡，在点态和以自我为中心的共形基线过于保守或昂贵的场景中成功到达目标，每步计算量远低于在线不确定性推理基线。

Conclusion: FCP框架通过场级共形化有效解决了共形预测在动态场景中的空间连贯性和扩展性问题，实现了高效、安全的运动规划，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Safe motion planning in dynamic environments requires reasoning about the uncertainty in predicted obstacle motion without sacrificing real-time performance. Existing conformal approaches conformalize a scalar score that aggregates per-obstacle prediction errors, losing spatial coherence and scaling poorly with scene density. We instead conformalize the entire predicted distance field at once. This functional conformal prediction (FCP) framework yields a distribution-free, field-level lower bound, from which safety follows uniformly: any trajectory satisfying the resulting constraint is certified safe, independent of how the control space is sampled. The key enabler is that the residual distance field is empirically low-rank and approximately time-invariant, which makes the bound decomposable in coefficient space. An envelope is fitted offline via functional PCA and a Gaussian-mixture inductive conformal procedure, then refined online by a lightweight adaptive functional conformal (AFCP) update on a low-dimensional vector. This keeps the per-step cost largely insensitive to obstacle count and retains long-run field coverage under distribution shift. We embed the envelope as a tightened safety constraint in a sampling-based model predictive controller, FCP-MPC. On the ETH--UCY pedestrian benchmarks and a dense 3D quadrotor task with up to 280 dynamic obstacles, FCP-MPC attains a favorable balance of safety, feasibility, and efficiency, reaching goals where pointwise and egocentric conformal baselines become too conservative or too expensive, while keeping per-step computation far below online uncertainty-reasoning baselines.

</details>


### [179] [From World Models to World Action Models: A Concise Tutorial for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00836)
*Xiaoxiong Zhang,Xiong Zeng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文以设计空间视角系统梳理了世界模型作为动作条件预测模型的概念范畴，将其分为观测空间和状态空间两类，并进一步提出世界动作模型及其四种代表性范式，为具身预测与控制提供了结构化分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在具身智能和生成仿真中应用日益广泛，但其概念范围在不同社区间仍然模糊不清，缺乏统一的定义和分类体系。作者希望通过厘清概念并提供结构化分类，推动该领域的发展。

Method: 提出世界模型的设计空间视角，将其定义为动作条件预测模型。将现有方法分为观测空间世界模型和状态空间世界模型，对比它们在视觉保真度、空间结构、物理可解释性和控制可用性上的权衡。进一步引入世界动作模型，总结出想象-执行、视频特征条件动作预测、联合视频-动作建模、辅助视频预测用于策略学习四种范式。

Result: 建立了世界模型与世界动作模型的概念框架和分类体系，明确了不同类别的特性与权衡。

Conclusion: 本文旨在澄清世界(动作)模型的概念范围，为具身预测与控制提供结构化的分类学参考，有助于不同社区对世界模型形成统一理解。

Abstract: World models are increasingly used in embodied intelligence and generative simulation, yet their scope remains ambiguous across communities. This tutorial presents a design-space view of world models as action-conditioned predictive models that estimate the future evolution of task-relevant observations or states. We categorize existing methods into observation-space and state-space world models, comparing their trade-offs in visual fidelity, spatial structure, physical interpretability, and control usability. We further introduce world action models, which connect predicted futures with executable robot actions, and summarize four representative paradigms: imagine-then-execute, video-feature-conditioned action prediction, joint video-action modeling, and auxiliary video prediction for policy learning. The goal of this tutorial is to clarify the conceptual scope of world (action) models and provide a structured taxonomy for embodied prediction and control.

</details>


### [180] [Beyond Line of Sight: Hybrid Validation of V2X Collective Perception in Complex Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00874)
*Markos Antonopoulos,Anastasia Bolovinou,Bill Roungas,Elena Daskalaki,Angelos Amditis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种V2X集体感知的概率框架与混合验证方法，通过贝叶斯融合算法扩展感知范围，结合CARLA仿真与车辆在环实验，在环形交叉口场景中将视野覆盖率提升260%，召回率从0.82提升至0.94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X集体感知缺乏统一概率框架和可重复的混合验证方法，难以兼顾感知不确定性表达与真实场景验证，阻碍了协同自动驾驶的安全认证部署。

Method: 提出贝叶斯融合算法，将多智能体异构传感器观测融合为概率占用网格，每个网格单元编码占用似然与不确定性；构建CARLA仿真与车辆在环的混合测试框架。

Result: 在环形交叉口场景中，六智能体CP相比单车感知视野覆盖率提升260%，占用单元召回率从0.82提升至0.94（标称定位条件下）。

Conclusion: 该方法为CP系统提供了可重复、可解释的验证基础，支持协同自动驾驶的安全认证部署。

Abstract: This paper introduces a probabilistic framework and hybrid validation methodology for V2X-enabled Collective Perception (CP) in complex traffic scenarios. The proposed Bayesian fusion algorithm extends the perceptual horizon of connected and autonomous vehicles by integrating heterogeneous sensor observations from multiple agents into a shared probabilistic occupancy grid. Each cell of this grid encapsulates both occupancy likelihood and uncertainty, enabling explainable and trustworthy situational awareness beyond the ego vehicle's field of view. To bridge the gap between simulation and real-world evaluation, a hybrid testing framework is developed, combining CARLA-based virtual environments with vehicle-in-the-loop experimentation. Experimental results in a roundabout scenario demonstrate a 260 percent increase in field-of-view coverage and a rise in occupied-cell recall from 0.82 (ego-only) to 0.94 (six-agent CP) under nominal localization conditions. Overall, the proposed approach provides a reproducible and interpretable foundation for validating CP systems, supporting the safe and certifiable deployment of cooperative autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [181] [AMBUSH: Collaborative Capture in Complex Environments with Neural Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01029)
*Junfeng Chen,YinHang Luo,Xinyi Wang,Junrui Li,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"伏击"策略的混合蒙特卡洛树搜索（H-MCTS）方法，使多个较慢的追捕者在复杂障碍环境中高效捕获更快、更智能的逃避者。通过参数化伏击策略、长时规划及神经网络加速，在仿真与硬件实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有追捕方法多局限于无障碍或稀疏规则障碍环境，缺乏在复杂环境中利用慢速追捕者捕获快速智能逃避者的有效策略。

Method: 1) 设计参数化伏击策略（含离散与连续参数），考虑拓扑特性、截断视线可见性、速度比及有限捕获范围；2) 提出H-MCTS算法优化参数，实现长时规划；3) 离线训练神经网络加速器，预测参数得分以替代H-MCTS中的rollout过程，加速在线规划。

Result: 在仿真与硬件实验中，该方法成功捕获了速度两倍于追捕者、以及由人类控制的逃避者，验证了其高效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 仅靠"伏击"策略即可使多个慢速追捕者在复杂环境中高效捕获更快、更智能的逃避者，神经网络加速能在保证规划质量的同时显著提升规划速度。

Abstract: Collaborative capture of dynamic targets is common in nature as an essential strategy for weaker species against the strong. Similar concepts have shown to be useful for numerous robotic applications, such as security and surveillance, search and rescue. However, most existing works focus on analytical and geometric solutions or end-to-end reinforcement learning methods, which are largely constrained to obstacle-free environments or scenarios with sparse, regularly distributed obstacles. This work tackles the problem from a unique perspective: the renowned strategy of``ambush'' alone would suffice for multiple slower pursuers to capture one faster evader with different levels of intelligence efficiently in complex environments. A parameterized strategy of ambush (including discrete and continuous parameters) is designed first, which takes into account the topological properties of the workspace, the truncated line-of-sight visibility, the relative speed ratio and the limited capture range. Then, a Hybrid Monte Carlo Tree Search (H-MCTS) algorithm is proposed to optimize the associated parameters through long-term planning, enabling the identification of highly promising parameters for future capture. Lastly, the neural acceleration is trained offline to learn the ranking of different choices of parameters across various environments, and to directly predict scores, replacing the rollout process in H-MCTS. The neural acceleration is adopted during online H-MCTS to accelerate the planning procedure while guaranteeing the planning quality. Its efficiency and effectiveness are validated in extensive simulations and hardware experiments, against evaders with different capabilities and intelligence levels, including two-times higher velocity and human-controlled behavior.

</details>


### [182] [DART-VLN: Test-Time Memory Decay and Anti-Loop Regularization for Discrete Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01043)
*Shaoheng Zhang,Zhichen Li,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-VLN 是一种无需训练的测试时控制框架，通过记忆衰减和反循环正则化来提升离散视觉-语言导航（VLN）中记忆读取和动作选择的可靠性，在 R2R 和 REVERIE 数据集上实现了更短路径、更少回溯和更优导航效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆型离散 VLN 代理在测试时面临两个主要失效模式：记忆读取时存在过时历史证据，动作选择时存在低效的本地回溯，导致导航性能不稳定。

Method: 提出 DART-VLN 框架，包含两个组件：1) 测试时记忆衰减（Test-Time Memory Decay），通过读取侧重加权规则抑制过时和冗余证据；2) 反循环正则化（Anti-Loop Regularization），通过轻量级下一跳惩罚机制阻止立即回退动作。整个框架无需训练，不引入新参数。

Result: 在 R2R 和 REVERIE 数据集上，仅使用记忆衰减即可稳定提升读取侧性能；衰减+反循环联合使用在关键场景下实现了更短的轨迹、更低的运行时间和更优的导航性能，取得了质量-效率的最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 适度且无需重新训练的测试时控制能够显著提升记忆型离散 VLN 导航的可靠性和效率，为现有 frozen backbone 提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: Memory-based discrete vision-language navigation (VLN) agents must act under partial observability, yet even strong frozen backbones remain vulnerable at test time. Two common failure modes are stale historical evidence at memory readout and inefficient local backtracking during action selection. We present DART-VLN, a training-free test-time control framework for discrete VLN. DART-VLN combines Test-Time Memory Decay, a read-side memory reweighting rule that suppresses stale and redundant evidence without rewriting stored content, with Anti-Loop Regularization, a lightweight next-hop penalty that discourages immediate reversals during action selection. The framework introduces no new learnable parameters and leaves the learned backbone unchanged. Experiments on R2R and REVERIE show a consistent pattern: decay-only provides stable read-side gains, while decay+anti-loop achieves the best overall quality-efficiency trade-off, yielding shorter trajectories, lower runtime, and improved navigation performance in key settings. Behavioral analysis further confirms that anti-loop regularization reduces local backtracking and improves path efficiency under frozen backbones. Overall, the results show that modest test-time control can make memory-based discrete VLN more reliable and efficient without retraining.

</details>


### [183] [Robots Ask the Way: Communication-Enabled Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01044)
*Valentino Sacco,Luca Scofano,Indro Spinelli,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CommNav框架，让机器人在多人家居环境中通过主动与人交流获取目标位置信息，在Habitat 3.0c仿真平台上实现10%的成功率提升，且对自然语言具有高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交导航方法仅关注反应式避障和轨迹调整，缺乏通过人机通信主动获取信息的机制，导致在多人家居环境中难以高效定位特定个体。

Method: 提出Communication-enabled Social Navigation (CommNav)任务，在Habitat 3.0基础上扩展出Habitat 3.0c仿真环境，加入信息交换协议；设计COMM通信模块，并采用通信预训练任务应对间歇性交互信号；分别使用LLM生成指令和人类口语指令进行训练与评估。

Result: COMM模块使SOTA社交导航模型的Episode Success提升10个百分点；通信预训练有效解决稀疏交互信号问题；导航策略对自然口语指令具有高鲁棒性，成功率与完美结构化数据模型无显著差异。

Conclusion: 明确的人-机器人通信显著增强多目标导航性能；COMM模块及预训练策略有效；所提方法在真实人类语言场景下表现稳健。

Abstract: Assistive autonomous robots operating in multi-agent environments require efficient strategies to locate specific individuals among multiple residents. Current social navigation methods focus on reactive collision avoidance and trajectory adaptation, but lack mechanisms to proactively gather information through human-robot communication.
  We introduce Communication-enabled Social Navigation (CommNav). In this novel task, robotic agents actively seek assistance from residents to locate target individuals by requesting information about recent sightings, locations, and movements.
  To evaluate CommNav, we extend Habitat 3.0 to create Habitat 3.0c, a communication-enabled variant supporting multi-human environments with information exchange protocols. Adding our communication module (COMM) to a state-of-the-art social navigation model yields a 10 percentage-point improvement in Episode Success. We further investigate the transition from structured data to natural language by evaluating models trained on LLM-generated instructions and on colloquial instructions collected from a human study.
  Our experiments reveal that: (i) explicit human-robot communication substantially enhances multi-person navigation performance; (ii) pre-training COMM on a communication pretext task effectively addresses the challenge of occasional interaction signals; and (iii) the navigation policy is highly robust to natural, colloquial human language, achieving an episode success statistically similar to the model using perfect structured data.

</details>
