### [184] [AutoSpeed: Annotation-Free Stage-Adaptive Motion Speed Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01051)
*Qingda Hu,Ziheng Qiu,Jieru Zhao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AutoSpeed框架，让机器人根据任务阶段自动调整动作速度，无需速度/阶段标注，通过频域速度调制缩短执行时间并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习的视觉运动策略固定了执行速度和预测时间窗口，无法根据任务阶段的难易程度灵活调整速度，限制了灵活性和任务吞吐量。

Method: 将不同速度的未来轨迹作为候选优化目标，用复合代价（权衡预测误差与预测窗口长度）评估，策略朝最小代价候选优化。速度调制通过离散余弦变换在频域实现平滑的非整数缩放。

Result: 大幅缩短任务执行时间，同时提升成功率；推断的动作速度与任务阶段高度对应。

Conclusion: AutoSpeed是一种模型无关的框架，能让现有视觉运动策略实现阶段自适应速度调节，提升效率与性能。

Abstract: Different stages of manipulation tasks exhibit varying levels of difficulty, suggesting stage-dependent motion speeds and temporal prediction horizons. However, existing IL-based visuomotor policies typically imitate the execution speed of expert demonstrations and operate with a fixed temporal prediction horizon, limiting flexibility and overall task throughput. In this paper, we introduce AutoSpeed, a model-agnostic learning framework that enables existing visuomotor policies to predict trajectories with stage-adaptive motion speeds, without requiring speed or stage annotations. We treat future trajectories at different speeds as candidate optimization targets, evaluate each candidate using a composite cost that trades off prediction error against prediction horizon, and optimize the policy toward the minimum-cost candidate. With a fixed-length action sequence, speed modulation adjusts the effective temporal prediction horizon: simple stages are executed faster with a longer prediction horizon, whereas complex stages are executed more slowly with a shorter prediction horizon. Specifically, we implement speed modulation in the frequency domain via the discrete cosine transform (DCT), which enables smooth, non-integer speed scaling and thus preserves motion continuity. Extensive evaluations show that AutoSpeed substantially reduces task execution time while also improving success rates. Under the AutoSpeed framework, the inferred motion speeds exhibit a strong correspondence with task stages.

</details>


### [185] [RoboWorld: Fast and Reliable Neural Simulators for Generalist Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01060)
*Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,JaeHyeok Doo,Sungdong Kim,Minjoon Seo,Hyungmok Son,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWorld是一个自动化评估流水线，结合快速自回归视频世界模型与任务进度感知的VLM评分器，通过Step Forcing技术减少训练-测试不匹配，实现与真实机器人评估高度一致（Pearson's r=0.989, Spearman's ρ=0.970）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型作为评估通用机器人策略的替代方案具有潜力，但存在生成rollout不可靠（因世界模型误差）和推理速度慢（限制大规模吞吐量）两大挑战。

Method: 提出RoboWorld评估流水线，包含：(1) 快速自回归视频世界模型；(2) 任务进度感知的VLM评分器；(3) Step Forcing技术，通过结合锚定上下文和单步自前向上下文来减少训练-测试不匹配，同时保持动作-观察动态。

Result: 与真实机器人评估高度对齐：Pearson's r = 0.989，Spearman's ρ = 0.970，跨越多个任务和环境。

Conclusion: RoboWorld能可靠地替代真实机器人评估，兼顾效率与准确性，为大规模机器人策略评估提供了可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Video world models are emerging as a scalable alternative for evaluating generalist robot policies, bypassing the physical constraints and engineering burdens of real-world deployment. However, evaluating policies with video world models remains challenging, as world-model errors can make generated rollouts unreliable and slow inference limits large-scale throughput. We introduce RoboWorld, an automated evaluation pipeline that pairs a fast autoregressive video world model with a task-progress-aware vision-language model scoring. To enable reliable long-horizon autoregressive world-model rollouts, we propose Step Forcing, which combines anchored and one-step self-forwarded contexts to reduce train--test mismatch while preserving action--observation dynamics. Together, these components enable RoboWorld to align strongly with real-world robot evaluation across tasks and environments, achieving Pearson's r = 0.989 and Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.970.

</details>


### [186] [Human-Centric Transferable Tactile Pre-Training for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01067)
*Chi Zhang,Penglin Cai,Ziheng Xi,Haoqi Yuan,Hao Luo,Wanpeng Zhang,Sipeng Zheng,Chaoyi Xu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出H-Tac大规模触觉-动作数据集（160小时、300+任务、135k片段），并基于此提出可迁移触觉预训练（TTP）系统，通过统一触觉与动作空间、未来触觉预测专家等机制，实现从人类数据到机器人任务的触觉预训练，显著提升精细操作与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉数据集规模小、接触覆盖窄，且视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在触觉模态上缺乏动力学感知的预训练，限制了精细操作任务的性能上限。

Method: 1）构建H-Tac大规模触觉-动作数据集（160小时人类视频、300+任务、135k片段）；2）提出TTP（可迁移触觉预训练）框架，在预训练和后训练阶段统一触觉与动作空间以保留迁移知识；3）利用触觉专家进行未来触觉预测，显式建模接触动力学与物理交互。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验上，模型展现出优越的性能、鲁棒的泛化能力和精细操作能力。

Conclusion: TTP通过人类到机器人的迁移，为可扩展的触觉预训练铺平了道路。

Abstract: As an essential modality for dexterous and contact-rich tasks, tactile sensing provides precise force feedback that cannot be reliably inferred from vision. However, limited by hardware and data collection systems, existing datasets with tactility remain small in scale and narrow in contact coverage. Meanwhile, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with tactile modality are constrained on dynamics-agnostic post-training, which limits the performance ceiling on downstream tasks. In this paper, we present H-Tac, a large-scale tactile-action dataset with 160-hour egocentric human videos containing more than 300 tasks and 135k episodes. Building upon this, we propose Transferable Tactile Pre-Training (TTP), a system of tactile-based pre-training on human data for fine-grained robotic tasks. To bridge the gap between humans and robots, we use unified tactile and action spaces throughout the pre-training and post-training phases, preserving prior knowledge during human-to-robot transfer. By leveraging a tactile expert for future tactile prediction, our framework explicitly models the contact dynamics and precise physical interactions. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate that our model achieves superior performance, exhibiting robust generalization and fine-grained manipulation capabilities. TTP paves the way for scalable tactile pre-training via human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [187] [Where Am I? Semantic Map Grounding via Vision-Language Models for Multi-Modal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01079)
*Suraj Borate,Aarav Shah,Madhu Vadali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将机器人定位重构为语义推理任务，使用微调后的Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型（+LoRA+轻量回归头）融合相机图像、LiDAR扫描和语义网格地图，直接预测连续位姿坐标。在自定义数据集上达到96.75%完整位姿精度，并展现出跨模态互补性和对未见物体/不完整地图的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何方法在GPS缺失的室内环境中鲁棒性有限，而人类依靠物体级语义线索进行定位。作者希望探索视觉-语言模型能否将定位转化为语义推理任务，从而绕过几何估计的局限性。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B作为骨干，通过LoRA微调，附加轻量回归头从最终隐藏状态直接预测连续位姿(x,y,θ)。训练采用复合位置-方向损失和课程学习策略。输入模态包括前置相机图像、极坐标LiDAR扫描和俯视语义网格地图。在包含120,112样本/527场景的定制Gazebo数据集上训练。

Result: 在分布内测试集（18,017样本）上：位置精度98.23%、方向精度98.00%、完整位姿精度96.75%，平均位置误差0.11m，平均朝向误差5.7°，推理速度0.62s/样本。在7个未见物体类别上位置精度仅下降7.2个百分点至90.99%。不完整地图经微调后恢复至93.72%。消融实验表明，相机无可见物体时LiDAR维持92.33%精度（无LiDAR时仅70.74%）。

Conclusion: VLM能够有效将机器人定位作为语义推理任务解决，具备良好的泛化性和跨模态鲁棒性。LiDAR在相机语义失效时提供可靠的后备信号，证明了多模态融合的必要性和互补性。

Abstract: We address robot localization in GPS-denied indoor environments by reframing it as a semantic reasoning task rather than a geometric estimation problem. Motivated by how humans localize using object-level cues and labeled maps, we ask whether a vision-language model, given a front camera image, a polar LiDAR scan, and a top-down semantic grid map, can infer the robot pose. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL-7B with LoRA and attach a lightweight regression head that predicts continuous pose coordinates (x, y, theta) directly from the final hidden state, bypassing text generation. Training uses a composite position-and-direction loss with curriculum learning on a custom Gazebo dataset of 120,112 samples and 527 scenes. On the in-distribution test set of 18,017 samples, the model achieves 98.23 percent position accuracy, 98.00 percent direction accuracy, 96.75 percent full pose accuracy, a mean position error of 0.11 m, and a mean orientation error of 5.7 degrees at 0.62 s per sample. Position accuracy drops by only 7.2 percentage points on seven unseen object categories, reaching 90.99 percent, supporting semantic spatial reasoning rather than appearance memorization. With incomplete maps, fine-tuning recovers performance to 93.72 percent position accuracy, showing adaptability to stale or partial map information. Two ablations highlight cross-modal complementarity. Without LiDAR, using only camera and map inputs, position accuracy remains 95.06 percent, only 3.2 percentage points below the full system. However, when the camera sees no visible objects in a wall-facing view, LiDAR sustains 92.33 percent position accuracy, compared with 70.74 percent when neither LiDAR nor visible objects are available. This shows that LiDAR becomes the primary localization signal when camera semantics are unavailable and provides a reliable fallback under occlusion or sparse layouts.

</details>


### [188] [ROSA: A Robotics Foundation Model Serving System for Robot Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01088)
*Wenqi Jiang,Jason Clemons,Rowland O'Flaherty,Hugo Hadfield,Alperen Degirmenci,Shuran Song,Yashraj Narang,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSA是一个面向机器人工厂的RFM服务系统，采用共享GPU池服务、多模型管线支持以及工厂目标驱动的调度策略，相比传统专用服务系统，工厂生产力提升最高达12.06倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFM服务系统基于单机器人-单模型假设，将推理视为边缘计算问题，无法满足工厂场景中多机器人协同、多模型管线、能效与GPU利用率优化等需求。

Method: 提出了三个关键设计原则：1) 共享GPU池服务，机器人通过网络访问服务器级GPU；2) 机器人感知的编程抽象与系统设计，支持多模型管线、任务级性能需求和故障处理；3) 工厂目标驱动的调度，优化SLO限定的工厂生产力而非单请求延迟。基于Ray Serve、vLLM、PyTorch和JAX实现。

Result: 在实际机器人和大规模合成负载上评估，ROSA相比传统专用服务系统将工厂生产力提升最高达12.06倍。

Conclusion: ROSA通过共享GPU池、多模型管线支持和工厂目标导向调度，有效提升了机器人工厂的整体生产力。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models (RFMs) are making general-purpose robots increasingly practical for factory deployments. While RFM serving systems are central to this vision, existing systems are largely shaped by a single-robot, single-model assumption: inference is treated as an edge-computing problem handled by an on-robot or dedicated nearby GPU, and the serving objective is to minimize the latency of a single action model. In this paper, we propose ROSA, an RFM serving system for robot factories designed around three key principles. First, ROSA adopts shared GPU-pool serving, allowing a fleet of robots to access powerful server-class GPUs over the network in order to improve inference performance, battery duration, and GPU utilization. Second, ROSA provides a robotics-aware programming abstraction and system design that supports multi-model pipelines, per-task performance requirements, and failure handling. Third, ROSA uses factory-objective-driven scheduling to maximize SLO-qualified factory productivity rather than minimizing individual request latency. We implement ROSA on top of Ray Serve for distributed orchestration, with vLLM, PyTorch, and JAX as model-serving backends, and evaluate it on both real robots and synthetic large-scale workloads. The results show that ROSA improves factory productivity by up to 12.06x over conventional dedicated serving systems.

</details>


### [189] [Technical Report: Asynchronous Distributed Trajectory Estimation of Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01106)
*Adam Pooley,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种异步块坐标下降算法用于分布式轨迹估计，能在异步通信和计算下实现指数级收敛，减少96.9%的通信量，误差比现有方法低64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式轨迹估计算法通常不考虑智能体之间通信和计算的异步性，而实际应用中异步是常见现象，因此需要一种能够处理异步的分布式轨迹估计算法。

Method: 提出异步块坐标下降算法，智能体团队在滑动窗口上观测并估计机器人状态，求解最大后验估计的近似问题，引入近似以消除通信冗余。

Result: 近似引入的误差可忽略不计，消除高达96.9%的智能体间通信；证明了迭代指数级收敛到最优估计；仿真显示误差比当前最优算法低64%；真实机器人实验验证了对跨三个数量级延迟的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该异步块坐标下降算法有效解决了异步场景下的分布式轨迹估计问题，兼具高效通信、强鲁棒性和理论收敛保证。

Abstract: Distributed trajectory estimation arises in many applications across robotics, but existing implementations typically do not consider asynchrony in agents' communications and computations. Therefore, we propose an asynchronous block coordinate descent algorithm for distributed trajectory estimation. We consider a team of agents that observes a team of robots and estimates their states over a sliding window. The agents solve an approximation of the maximum a posteriori estimation problem, which we derive. We show this approximation introduces negligible errors and eliminates up to 96.9% of communications among agents. Next, we prove that agents' iterates converge exponentially fast to the optimal estimate of the robots' states. Simulations show that this approach has up to 64% less error than a comparable state-of-the-art algorithm. Experiments on mobile robots show the robustness of this approach to delays whose lengths span three orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [190] [FAR: Failure-Aware Retry for Test-Time Recovery and Continual Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01111)
*Haoran Hao,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Failure-Aware Retry (FAR)框架，让机器人从测试时的失败中学习，通过失败对比偏好适配和轻量级动作扰动实现自主恢复，在仿真和真实实验中分别提升17.6%和11.7%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在真实环境中部署时难免失败，而简单重试会重复错误，现有恢复方法又依赖人工干预，亟需一种能自主从失败中学习并改进的框架。

Method: FAR包含两个核心组件：1) Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation，从失败中构建偏好学习数据，引导策略远离失败行为；2) 在重试过程中加入轻量级动作扰动以促进局部探索。同时将成功的恢复轨迹纳入训练循环进行持续策略改进。

Result: 在仿真环境中，FAR相比标准扩散策略平均提升17.6%的成功率；在真实世界任务中提升11.7%。在持续策略改进阶段，通过利用有信息的失败案例，显著提升了数据效率。

Conclusion: FAR框架证明了机器人可以通过测试时的失败自主学习和调整，在无需人工干预的情况下显著提升任务成功率与鲁棒性，同时具备更高的数据利用效率。

Abstract: Robot policies inevitably encounter failures when deployed in real environments. Naive retries often repeat the same mistakes, while many existing recovery methods rely on human intervention. In this paper, we propose Failure-Aware Retry (FAR), a framework that enables robots to learn from previous failures at test time, adapt their behavior accordingly, and eventually complete the task autonomously. FAR combines Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation, which constructs preference learning data from failures to steer the policy away from previously unsuccessful behaviors, with lightweight action perturbations during retries to encourage local exploration. We further incorporate successful recovery trajectories into a training loop for continual policy improvement. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks show that FAR substantially improves success rates and robustness, with average gains of 17.6% over the standard diffusion policy in simulation and 11.7% in the real world. In addition, FAR significantly improves data efficiency under both reset and timestep budgets during continual policy improvement by exploiting informative failure cases.

</details>


### [191] [Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01166)
*Zhiyi Li,Peilin Wu,Xiaoshen Han,Ruojin Cai,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化4D潜变量预测模型，通过在结构化潜空间中预测场景的3D结构演化，实现更强的3D一致性和多视图连贯性，在复杂操控任务、视觉泛化和真实机器人平台上均优于现有基于视频的规划方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频预测模型缺乏3D几何理解，难以进行精确的空间推理和物理一致性建模，限制了其在机器人任务中的应用。

Method: 提出结构化4D潜变量预测模型，在结构化潜空间中编码场景并预测其3D结构演化；结合条件观测和文本指令，将预测的未来场景通过目标条件逆动力学模块转换为可执行动作。

Result: 模型在视觉质量、3D一致性和多视图连贯性上显著优于基于视频的规划器；在复杂操控任务、视觉泛化及真实机器人平台上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 结构化4D潜变量预测模型作为一种规划器，能够生成具有强3D一致性的未来场景，有效提升机器人任务的规划性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: Video predictive models are emerging as a powerful paradigm in robotics, offering a promising path toward task generalization, long-horizon planning, and flexible decision-making. However, prevailing approaches often operate on 2D video sequences, inherently lacking the 3D geometric understanding necessary for precise spatial reasoning and physical consistency. We introduce a Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model, which predicts the evolution of a scene's 3D structure in a structured latent space conditioned on observations and textual instructions. Our representation encodes the scene holistically and can be decoded into diverse 3D formats, enabling a more complete and 3D consistent scene understanding. This structured 4D latent predictive model serves as a planner, generating future scenes that are translated into executable actions by a goal-conditioned inverse dynamics module. Experiments demonstrate that our model generates futures with strong visual quality, substantially better 3D consistency and multi-view coherence compared to state-of-the-art video-based planners. Consequently, our full planning pipeline achieves superior performance on complex manipulation tasks, exhibits robust generalization to novel visual conditions, and proves effective on real-world robotic platforms. Our website is available at https://structured-4d-model.github.io/.

</details>


### [192] [FastBridge: Closing the Model-Based Realization Gap in Safety Filters on 3D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01200)
*Tscholl Dario,Nakka Yashwanth Kumar,Gunter Brian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的非线性、执行器感知的安全过滤器，利用完整四旋翼动力学和高相对度碰撞锥指数CBF，相比现有方法减少47%的轨迹急动度并提速2.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS安全滤波器使用降阶模型（如单/双积分器），忽略执行器限制并假设加速度可瞬间实现，导致实际飞行中安全性不足。

Method: 推导了高相对度碰撞锥指数控制障碍函数（CBF）以及基于前向仿真备份策略的备份CBF，在完整四旋翼动力学下通过QP实现执行器感知的安全过滤。

Result: 相比现有3DGS安全滤波器，轨迹急动度降低47%，运行速度提升2.25倍，并在仿真和实物实验中验证了实时导航性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法在感知驱动的杂乱环境中实现了更平滑、更快速的安全导航，且满足了实际执行器约束。

Abstract: Fast quadrotor flight requires safe obstacle avoidance under tight onboard compute limits. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides a continuous, geometry-aware scene representation for perception-driven navigation, existing 3DGS safety filters use reduced-order models such as single- and double-integrators that ignore actuator limits and assume commanded accelerations are realized instantaneously. Building on an analytic collision cone barrier for 3DGS, we introduce a nonlinear, actuator-aware safety filter enforced through the full quadrotor dynamics. We derive a high-relative-degree collision cone exponential CBF and a backup CBF that preserves QP feasibility under input constraints using a forward-simulated backup policy. Compared with a state-of-the-art 3DGS safety filter, our approach reduces trajectory jerk by 47% and runs 2.25 times faster. We validate the method in simulation and on hardware for real-time navigation in cluttered, perception-derived environments.

</details>


### [193] [Sensorless Four-Channel Control Architecture Using Inverse Dynamics Modeling for Human-Scale Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01201)
*Amir Noohian,Dylan Miller,Justin Valentine,Alan Lynch,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逆动力学建模的无传感器四通道遥操作架构，在WAM平台上验证了其相比传统方案在力/位跟踪、操作力及阻抗传递方面的优势，并通过开门实验证明了其在人尺度操作中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四通道遥操作架构在人尺度操作中受限于高惯性、建模困难以及对噪声敏感且昂贵的力/扭矩传感器，亟需一种无需外部传感器即可实现透明性的方法。

Method: 提出无传感器四通道架构，基于逆动力学建模替代力传感器，在定制WAM双边遥操作平台上实现并验证。

Result: 所提方法优于传统二/四通道方案及透明性增强方法，改善了力/位跟踪精度，降低了操作者努力，提高了最大可传输阻抗；开门案例进一步验证了实际人尺度操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于逆动力学的无传感器四通道架构可有效提升人尺度遥操作系统的透明性，无需依赖外部力传感器即可实现高性能控制。

Abstract: The four-channel teleoperation architecture is a well-established framework for achieving transparency in bilateral systems. However, its performance in human-scale teleoperation is limited by high inertia, modeling challenges, and reliance on noisy and costly force/torque sensors. This paper introduces a sensorless four-channel architecture based on inverse dynamics modeling. The controller is implemented and validated on a customized WAM bilateral teleoperation setup. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional two- and four-channel schemes as well as transparency-enhancement methods, improving position and force tracking, reducing operator effort, and increasing maximum transmittable impedance without external sensors. A door-opening case study involving sustained whole-body contact along the manipulator further demonstrates the effectiveness of the method in realistic human-scale manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [194] [FurnitureVLA: Learning Long-Horizon Bimanual Furniture Assembly with Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01212)
*Chenyang Ma,Yue Yang,Radu Corcodel,Siddarth Jain,Andrew Wu,Chiori Hori,Diego Romeres*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FurnitureVLA，首个系统研究真实尺寸双臂家具组装的VLA模型，通过进度增强的VLA、仿真管道和VR遥操作，在7个子任务、1550步的长时域任务中将仿真成功率从48%提升至80%，并成功迁移到真实机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人家具组装工作多局限于玩具级场景或单臂操作，缺乏对真实尺寸、双臂协同、长时域复杂组装任务的系统研究。

Method: 1) 形式化真实尺寸双臂家具组装任务；2) 构建可扩展的仿真管道用于专家数据生成和评估；3) 开发VR遥操作系统实现单人双臂控制收集真实演示；4) 提出进度增强的VLA模型，联合预测动作和连续进度信号，支持自动子任务切换并减少累加误差；5) 系统研究感知和控制设计因素对精度的影响。

Result: 在三种家具类型上，仿真成功率为80%（基线48%），设计因素研究额外提升21%；在真实Kinova Gen3平台上最困难任务仅下降16%。

Conclusion: FurnitureVLA证明了进度增强的VLA模型在真实尺寸双臂家具组装任务上的有效性，为机器人长时域复杂操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Current work on robot furniture assembly mostly focuses on toy-scale settings or single-arm manipulation. We introduce FurnitureVLA, the first systematic study of real-scale bimanual furniture assembly using Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). We formalize the task, develop a scalable simulation pipeline for expert data generation and evaluation, and build a VR teleoperation system for single-operator bimanual control to collect high-quality real-world demonstrations. To address extreme long-horizon assembly with up to 7 subtasks and 1550 control steps, we propose a progress-enhanced VLA, finetuned on semantically grounded subtasks, that jointly predicts actions and a continuous progress signal, enabling automatic subtask transitions and reducing compounding errors during inference. We further study perception and control design factors that critically affect precision in real-scale assembly. FurnitureVLA improves average simulation success from 48% to 80% compared to baselines across three furniture types, with an additional 21% gain from our design factor study. We validate on a real Kinova Gen3 platform with only 16% drop on the hardest task.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Constructive Alignment: Governing Preference Dynamics in Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00001)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"建构性对齐"范式，将AI对齐问题从满足静态偏好重新定义为控制人类偏好演化轨迹的控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法假设人类偏好是固定的，但这与心理学和行为经济学证据相矛盾——偏好具有层次性、动态性，且在与技术交互中被构建。随着AI系统越来越个性化和社会化，它们实际上在塑造人类的注意力、价值观和偏好。

Method: 借鉴行为经济学、心理学和建构主义社会理论，将偏好建模为随AI交互而演化的分层状态变量，并采用控制论框架统一建模系统行为与交互设计对世界状态和人类评价状态的联合影响。

Result: 提出了对齐的新目标：确保价值轨迹保持连贯性、反思性认可、认识论根基、抗操纵性，以及在不确定性下的赋能性。

Conclusion: 对齐的核心不是控制AI行为本身，而是调控AI系统对人类偏好演化的影响，从而将问题从"满足静态偏好"转向"治理长期价值形成过程"。

Abstract: Most approaches to AI alignment treat human preferences as fixed targets to be inferred and optimized. This assumption conflicts with extensive empirical evidence showing that preferences are layered, dynamic, and constructed through interaction--particularly with adaptive technologies. As AI systems become more persistent, personalized, and socially embedded, they increasingly participate in shaping what people attend to, value, and endorse over time. We introduce Constructive Alignment, a paradigm that reframes alignment as a control problem over evolving human preference trajectories rather than static preference satisfaction. Drawing on behavioral economics, psychology, and constructivist social theory, we model preferences as layered state variables that evolve under interaction with AI systems. We formalize this view using a control-theoretic framework in which system actions and interaction design jointly influence both world states and human evaluative states. We argue that alignment is not primarily about controlling AI behavior, but about regulating how AI systems influence the evolution of human preferences--ensuring that value trajectories remain coherent, reflectively endorsed, epistemically grounded, bounded against manipulation, and empowering under uncertainty. Alignment thus becomes a problem of governing long-term value formation rather than simply satisfying static preferences.

</details>


### [196] [Bounded Morality: Defining the Space of Moral Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00002)
*Max Kanwal,Caryn Tran,Patrick Mineault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有界道德"框架，将有限理性概念扩展到道德认知领域，论证道德问题在计算资源约束下存在广度与深度的不可回避权衡，不同伦理理论是适应不同需求模式的局部高效策略，而非竞争性的道德真理学说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道德认知模型将伦理理论视为固定规则或价值函数，忽视了智能体在解决道德问题时的计算资源有限性。作者希望将赫伯特·西蒙的有界理性思想引入道德认知，为有限智能体的道德问题计算需求提供形式化分析框架。

Method: 提出道德广度（道德相关实体范围）与道德深度（评估交互所需的推理整合程度）两个正交维度，论证有限资源下两者存在不可回避的权衡，从而定义可行的道德计算空间。在此空间内，不同伦理理论被重新解释为适应不同需求模式的局部高效策略。

Result: 该框架形式化定义了道德遗憾（moral regret）和约束下的道德进步（moral progress），并指出人工系统中的道德对齐（moral alignment）取决于道德推理能力的扩展与分配，而非对人类判断的直接模仿。

Conclusion: 道德认知不应被视为对固定伦理理论的遵守，而应被理解为在计算资源约束下的适应性问题解决过程。这对AI对齐有重要启示：对齐的关键不在于模仿人类输出，而在于赋予系统适当的道德推理扩展能力。

Abstract: Moral cognition has traditionally been modeled as adherence to fixed ethical theories--deontology, consequentialism, virtue ethics--implemented as static rules or value functions. We propose Bounded Morality, a formal framework for analyzing the computational demands of moral problems faced by finite agents. Extending Herbert Simon's notion of bounded rationality, we formalize moral situations along two orthogonal dimensions: moral breadth, the scope of entities treated as morally relevant, and moral depth, the inferential integration required to evaluate their interactions. Limited resources impose an unavoidable tradeoff between these dimensions, defining a feasible space of moral computation. Within this space, ethical theories correspond to locally efficient strategies adapted to different demand regimes rather than competing accounts of moral truth. The framework yields a formal notion of moral regret and moral progress under constraint, and implies that moral alignment in artificial systems depends on the scaling and allocation of moral reasoning capacity rather than on direct imitation of human judgments.

</details>


### [197] [The MMM Data Model -- A Normative Specification for Knowledge Interoperability in a Decentralisable Knowledge Commons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00032)
*Mathilde Noual*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MMM是一个面向跨学科协作的知识文档数据模型，结合少量规范性约束与自由文本标签，旨在替代传统文档，提升知识的结构化、更新、共享和复用能力，并在无需语义统一的前提下实现跨领域互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文档系统在结构化、更新、共享和复用知识方面存在局限，而现有形式化方法因优先考虑形式结构而牺牲了可用性和参与度，AI虽改变文档生产但未能提供统一便携的知识表达方案。

Method: MMM数据模型：结合少量规范性约束与自由文本标签的表达自由，设计上无需语义统一即可实现跨学科、跨应用和跨部署的互操作性。

Result: 通过参考实现和试点部署数据，证明了MMM的可实现性和早期可用性。

Conclusion: MMM在形式化与灵活性之间取得了平衡，为跨学科协作中的知识记录提供了一种实用、可互操作的新方案。

Abstract: Many information systems are built around documents: self-contained units optimised for print production and linear reading. While effective for large-scale dissemination, the document-centric organisation constrains how knowledge can be structured, updated, shared, and reused. Formal approaches address some of these limitations but struggle to achieve widespread contribution and adoption due to their prioritisation of formal structure over other system properties such as human usability and scope. AI systems are reshaping document production, but without providing a unified portable alternative to traditional documents for humans' expression and exchange of knowledge. This paper presents MMM, a data model for knowledge documentation that emerged from the practical needs of interdisciplinary collaborative research, and positioned here within a comparative analysis of the design space of information systems. MMM combines a small set of normative constraints with the expressive freedom of free-text labels. It is designed for interoperability across disciplines, applications and deployments without requiring semantic convergence. A reference implementation and pilot deployment data demonstrate implementability and early usability.

</details>


### [198] [Making Failure Safe: A Constrained, Verifiable Agent Framework for Open-Web Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00035)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个约束性、可验证的代理框架，将LLM输出的自由形式代码转换为类型化JSON收集器配置，通过六类型收集器分类法、模板约束、静态DAG执行和规则检查，实现可复用的低成本的确定性网页数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM和代理直接从自然语言生成网络爬虫并不可靠，存在依赖错误、选择器损坏、模式不匹配和页面结构异构等问题。

Method: 提出约束性可验证代理框架：(1)六类型收集器分类法；(2)模板和工具函数约束；(3)静态Airflow DAG执行；(4)基于规则的质量检查；(5)结构化反馈修正。将LLM输出从自由形式代码转换为类型化JSON配置。

Result: 在138个任务上验证了分类法支持基于描述的需求类型化；在80个独立源验证任务上，框架实现了零执行阶段LLM token消耗和最低平均墙钟时间。

Conclusion: 该框架以适中的一次性生成质量换取了可复用、确定性和可验证的执行路径，特别适合重复调度的开放网络数据采集场景，定位为低成本可复用的数据采集方案。

Abstract: LLMs and agents can generate web scrapers from natural-language requirements, but direct generation remains unreliable because of dependency errors, broken selectors, schema mismatches, and heterogeneous page structures. We propose a constrained, verifiable agent framework that shifts LLM output from free-form code to typed JSON collector configurations, combining a six-type collector taxonomy, template and utility-function constraints, static Airflow DAG execution, rule-based quality checking, and structured feedback correction. Experiments on 138 tasks show that the taxonomy supports description-based requirement typing, while confirming that stable instantiation requires completing source, field, and execution constraints beyond the initial description. On 80 independently source-verified tasks, the framework runs with zero execution-stage LLM tokens and the lowest average wall-clock time, trading moderate one-shot quality for a reusable, deterministic, and verifiable execution path suited to repeated scheduled collection. These results position the framework as a reusable, low-cost, and verifiable execution path for repeated open-web data collection.

</details>


### [199] [Solution space path planning for supporting en-route air traffic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00064)
*Yiyuan Zou,Wenying Lyu,Clark Borst*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为空中交通管制员设计的可解释、高效的冲突解脱路径规划算法，通过整合三种意图冲突检测方法与解空间路径规划框架，在MUAC Delta扇区场景下实现了平均3.69毫秒的计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路径规划算法与实际空中交通管制员需求之间存在严重脱节，缺乏可解释性、计算高效且专为人类决策设计的解决方案。

Method: 基于解空间框架，集成距离型、时间间隔型和区域型三种意图冲突检测方法，并提出了基于顶点（SSPPV）和基于边（SSPPE）两种解空间路径规划变体。

Result: SSPPV配合区域冲突检测方法表现最佳，在MUAC Delta扇区5海里网格场景下平均计算时间为3.69毫秒。

Conclusion: 所提算法在可解释性、计算效率和路径质量之间取得了良好平衡，为空中交通管制的实际应用提供了可行的决策支持方案。

Abstract: As technology advances, many path-planning algorithms have been proposed for Air Traffic Management, yet their operational adoption in tactical control remains limited, revealing a misalignment between algorithmic design priorities and air traffic controllers' needs. This underscores the need for decision-support solutions that are inherently interpretable, computationally efficient, and explicitly designed for human use. Focusing on this design challenge, this study develops a conflict-free path-planning algorithm for en-route Air Traffic Control (ATC) designed to be compatible with two guiding considerations: (1) the interpretability and flexibility offered by solution-space displays, which motivate constructing an algorithm that exposes all feasible safe actions and accommodates shifting optimization goals; and (2) the decision logic controllers naturally apply when enforcing operational constraints, such as separation standards, maneuverability limits, waypoint minimization, and routing practicality. Centered on these principles, the algorithm integrates three intent-based conflict detection methods -- distance-based, time-interval-based, and zone-based -- within a solution-space framework to identify conflict-free paths in computationally efficient ways. Additionally, vertex-based and edge-based search nodes are proposed for solution space path planning (SSPP), resulting in two variants -- SSPPV and SSPPE, respectively, which are evaluated in terms of computational speed and solution quality. Empirical results show that SSPPV paired with zone-based conflict detection achieves the best performance, computing paths in 3.69 ms on average in operational-relevant scenarios based on the Delta sector of the Maastricht Upper Area Control Centre (MUAC) using a 5 nmi grid.

</details>


### [200] [RareDxR1: Autonomous Medical Reasoning for Rare Disease Diagnosis Beyond Human Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00147)
*Deyang Jiang,Haoran Wu,Ziyi Wang,Yiming Rong,Yunlong Zhao,Ye Jin,Bo Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RareDxR1，一个端到端的、以推理为中心的大语言模型，用于直接从非结构化临床笔记进行开放域罕见病诊断。通过知识内化、自主进化学习、反思增强推理采样（RERS）和双层次课程强化学习，在多个基准上达到了最先进的诊断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病鉴别诊断是一项困难任务，现有AI方法（基于流水线的表型提取或检索增强生成）受限于预定义本体、检索瓶颈和缺乏诊断逻辑，导致关键信息丢失。

Method: 1) 渐进式端到端训练框架：结合知识内化与自主进化学习；2) 将碎片化罕见病知识直接内化到模型参数中，突破RAG和表型限制；3) 反思增强推理采样（RERS）：无需人工标注，从失败中学习专家级诊断轨迹；4) 双层次课程强化学习：逐步掌握罕见病诊断。

Result: 在多个基准上实现了最先进的诊断准确率，在开放域罕见病诊断中取得显著突破。

Conclusion: RareDxR1通过端到端推理中心和知识内化策略，有效克服了传统AI方法的局限性，为罕见病诊断提供了新范式。

Abstract: Rare disease differential diagnosis is a critical yet arduous clinical task, requiring physicians to identify precise phenotypes from complex, unstructured patient symptoms and execute intricate reasoning within a vast search space. However, existing AI approaches typically rely on pipeline-based phenotype extraction or retrieval-augmented generation, which suffer from critical information loss due to predefined ontologies, retrieval bottlenecks, and a lack of diagnostic logic. To address these challenges, we introduce RareDxR1, an end-to-end reasoning-centric large language model designed for open-domain rare disease diagnosis directly from unstructured clinical notes. We design a progressive end-to-end training framework by synergizing knowledge internalization with autonomous evolutionary learning, thereby bypassing reliance on structured phenotypes and closed-set decision-making. To overcome the limitations of RAG and phenotype restriction, we enabled the deep internalization of fragmented rare-disease knowledge directly into the model's parameters. Moreover, to bridge the gap between model generation and expert reasoning, we propose Reflection-Enhanced Reasoning Sampling (RERS), a strategy that synthesizes expert-level diagnostic trajectories by learning from failures without human annotation. Additionally, we propose a dual-level curriculum reinforcement learning approach for gradually mastering rare disease diagnosis. Experimental results demonstrate that RareDxR1 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across different benchmarks, marking a significant breakthrough in open-domain rare disease diagnosis. Our code and dataset will be publicly available.

</details>


### [201] [A Contextual-Bandit Oversight Game with Two-Sided Informational Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00155)
*Yunjin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI与人类之间存在双向私人信息时的运行时监督问题，提出一个包含双向不对称信息的情境赌博团队游戏模型，并给出了单轮最优解与近视行为策略之间的"可避免伤害区间"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的AI自主系统（如机器人或软件代理）检查了人类监督者无法直接评估的情境，导致双向信息不对称：人类私下知道自己的奖励函数，AI私下知道其提议行动的质量。现有模型（如CIRL和监督博弈）未能充分刻画这种双向不对称信息下的运行时监督问题。

Method: 基于CIRL和监督博弈，构建了一个带有双向不对称信息的情境赌博团队游戏（contextual-bandit team game），具有play/ask/trust/oversee交互界面。通过赌博机结构去除物理状态转移，从而在完整POMDP设定中无法得到的单轮精确刻画成为可能。给出了两个单轮刻画：团队最优解和行为学上的近视规则。

Result: 发现团队最优解与近视规则之间的差距是一个"可避免伤害区间"（slab of avoidable harm）：在这个区间内，AI私下知道提议的行动是有害的且应当关闭，但近视的人类由于信任先验而拒绝监督。这个差距是"不可信监督沟通"的代价（price of non-credible oversight communication）。

Conclusion: 通过被动学习和主动信号（带一期延迟的监督响应），该差距可以在多轮动态博弈中得到部分缓解。研究为理解AI系统的运行时人类监督在双向信息不对称下的局限性和改进方向提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study runtime human oversight of an AI agent when private information runs in both directions: the human privately knows her reward function, while the AI privately knows the quality of the action it proposes. This is the kind of asymmetry that arises naturally when an autonomous robot or software agent has inspected a situation its human supervisor cannot directly assess. Building on Cooperative Inverse Reinforcement Learning (CIRL) and the Oversight Game, we introduce a contextual-bandit team game with two-sided asymmetric information and a play/ask/trust/oversee interface. The bandit structure removes physical state transitions and thereby yields exact one-shot characterizations that would remain conjectural in the full POMDP setting, though the common belief remains a dynamically controlled state across rounds. We give two one-shot characterizations, a team optimum and a behaviorally natural myopic rule, whose gap is a slab of avoidable harm: a region in which the AI privately knows the proposed action is harmful and shutdown would help, yet a myopic human, trusting her prior, declines to oversee. We show this gap is the price of non-credible oversight communication, and give a partial analysis of how it resolves dynamically over repeated rounds through passive learning and active signaling with a one-period-lagged oversight response.

</details>


### [202] [Constructing Epistemic AI Literacy: Detecting Epistemic Aims and Processes in Student-AI Co-Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00211)
*Mengqian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出"Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL)"概念框架，将AI素养重新定义为一种过程导向的认知现象。通过对人-AI协作编程的大规模对话数据分析，发现78.8%的学生与GenAI互动缺乏认知投入，仅11.1%表现出高认知参与。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI素养研究多关注技能掌握，忽视了在编程等复杂任务中，学生与生成式AI互动时所需的认知思考（如构建查询、评估输出、调节策略）这一核心过程。作者希望将AI素养重新定义为一种认知现象，理解学生在人-AI协作中如何进行认知层面的学习。

Method: 基于AIR认知框架（认知目标、认知理想、可靠认知过程），对大规模人-AI协作编程对话数据集进行分析，识别认知目标和认知过程的可观测维度，包括掌握导向目标、外包、寻求解释、寻求验证、提示监控和认知辩护等策略。

Result: 78.8%的学生-GenAI互动依赖非掌握导向目标以及外包、寻求验证等不可靠认知策略；仅11.1%的互动表现出高认知参与，即掌握导向目标与认知辩护等高级认知策略结合，形成更可靠的认知过程。

Conclusion: 学生在使用生成式AI进行编程学习时普遍缺乏认知AI素养，多数互动停留在表面策略层面。研究呼吁关注AI素养的认知维度，并提供了在互动数据中操作化这些构念的可扩展方法。

Abstract: Epistemic thinking plays a central role in students' learning processes when applying generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), particularly in programming contexts where learners must construct queries, evaluate and validate AI-generated outputs, and regulate problem-solving strategies. This study introduces the conceptual framework of Epistemic AI Literacy (EAIL), reframing AI literacy as a process-oriented epistemic phenomenon that emerges through dynamic human-AI interactions across different domains. Drawing on the AIR (epistemic aims, ideals and reliable epistemic processes) framework, this study examines how epistemic aims and epistemic processes are enacted in GenAI-supported co-programming activities and explores scalable approaches for operationalizing these constructs in interaction data. Using a large dialogue dataset of human-AI co-programming, this study identifies observable dimensions of epistemic aims (i.e., mastery-oriented aims) and epistemic processes (i.e., outsourcing, explanation seeking, verification seeking, prompt monitoring, and epistemic justification). The results reveal a prevalent lack of EAIL, with 78.8% of student-GenAI interactions relying on non-mastery-oriented aims and less reliable epistemic strategies like outsourcing and verification-seeking. Conversely, only 11.1% of interactions showed high epistemic engagement, where mastery-oriented aims were coupled with advanced epistemic strategies like epistemic justification in a more reliable epistemic process.

</details>


### [203] [From Signals to Structure: How Memory Architecture Drives Language Emergence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00233)
*Yashar Talebirad,Eden Redman,Ali Parsaee,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Lewis信号博弈中，使用LLM智能体研究五种记忆架构，发现记忆架构比信道容量更重要，拥有持久私人笔记本的智能体达到最佳协调效果，而信道容量本身不能单独预测协调结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索两个智能体如何从头发明共享语言，理解记忆架构和信道容量在语言形成中的作用。

Method: 在Lewis信号博弈中，使用LLM智能体测试五种记忆架构，在不同信道配置下进行实验，比较协调表现。

Result: 记忆架构比信道容量更重要；持久私人笔记本架构在容量=25时达到最佳协调（0.867±0.023）；无状态智能体在中等容量达到峰值后随词汇增长退化；信道容量=8是最脆弱点，而非最优。

Conclusion: 信道容量不能单独预测协调结果；记忆架构决定智能体是否能将交互历史转化为稳定约定；两个维度都需要理解信号如何变成语言。

Abstract: How do two agents invent a shared language from scratch? In a Lewis signaling game, a sender and receiver must coordinate on a code using only their interaction history. We study five memory architectures across varying channel configurations with LLM agents and find that memory architecture matters more than channel capacity. Agents with a persistent private notebook benefit from surplus channel capacity and avoid the high-capacity collapse seen in stateless agents, achieving the most reliable coordination ($0.867 \pm 0.023$ at capacity = 25). Stateless agents peak at moderate capacity and then degrade as the vocabulary grows beyond what a rolling context window can track The notebook externalizes learned conventions, freeing agents from having to re-derive codes each round. An information bottleneck-inspired argument predicts an optimal capacity equal to the number of objects. Instead, the bottleneck (capacity = 8) proves to be a fragility point, and surplus capacity is generally better. We show that channel capacity alone cannot predict coordination; memory architecture determines whether agents turn interaction history into stable conventions, and both dimensions are needed to understand how signals become language.

</details>


### [204] [Seed2.0 Model Card: Towards Intelligence Frontier for Real-World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00248)
*Bytedance Seed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Seed2.0是一个模型系列，通过构建基于用户真实需求的评估系统，重点解决长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大挑战，在推理、视觉理解和搜索能力上达到领先水平，初步展现出处理复杂现实任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别用户的真实需求，构建可靠且前瞻的评估系统，推动模型解决复杂的现实世界任务。

Method: 基于用户真实需求选择并抽象基准测试，构建评估系统；针对长尾知识和复杂指令跟随两大挑战进行优化。

Result: Seed2.0在推理智能、视觉理解和搜索能力方面达到世界领先水平，初步具备处理复杂现实任务的能力。

Conclusion: Seed2.0在解决复杂现实任务方面取得了有意义的进展，能够为亿万用户提供更大的价值。

Abstract: We present Seed2.0, a model series that takes a meaningful step toward solving complex, real-world tasks. Our approach begins with identifying users' genuine needs and constructing a reliable, forward-looking evaluation system by selecting and abstracting benchmarks grounded in these needs and in realistic, complex scenarios. Guided by this evaluation system, Seed2.0 targets two persistent challenges, long-tail knowledge and complex instruction following, substantially improving the model's reliability on intricate, long-horizon tasks. Beyond these, Seed2.0 delivers world-leading reasoning intelligence, visual understanding, and search capabilities that address the most common needs of a broad user base. Through extensive real-world use cases documented in this model card, we demonstrate that Seed2.0 begins to exhibit the ability to handle initial complex real-world tasks, delivering greater value to hundreds of millions of users.

</details>


### [205] [Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269)
*Edward Y. Chang,Longling Geng,Emily J. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic事务处理(ATP)模型，将生成动作视为不可信提案，通过确定性约束集C进行准入控制。实现系统Mnemosyne包含追加日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿等机制，证明了四项安全属性和有界反应性修复保证。实验显示开销低于6%，局部修复比全局重算少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM、求解器和智能体团队生成的工作流动作虽语法有效，但可能存在过期、不可行、冲突或破坏修复证据等问题，亟需一种形式化的事务模型来确保生成动作的正确性和安全性。

Method: 提出ATP事务模型：将生成动作视为不可信提案，通过确定性约束集C进行准入控制。实现Mnemosyne运行时系统，包含追加只写日志、有效状态投影、依赖安全补偿和活跃承诺记录，并设计局部因果反应协议(LCRP)处理意外中断。

Result: 在9个伪造测试中拒绝目标违规同时接受有效工作，投影和验证开销低于6%，局部修复比全局重算操作数少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过将生成层与运行层分离，提交后状态正确性不再依赖于提议层的胜任力、诚实性或学习能力，为AI生成工作流提供了一种形式化安全保障机制。

Abstract: LLMs, solvers, and agent teams increasingly generate workflow actions, repairs, and plans, but a generated action may be syntactically valid yet stale, infeasible, conflicting, or destructive of the evidence that triggered a repair. We introduce Agentic Transaction Processing (ATP), a transaction model that treats generated actions as untrusted proposals until they pass deterministic admission under a declared, executable constraint set C. The principle is two-sided: a proposal is not truth, and no proposal foresees every disruption: anything may propose, but only the runtime admits and commits, and when an unforeseen disruption strikes it repairs reactively within bounds rather than trusting a fresh proposal. Relative to C, committed-state correctness becomes independent of the competence, honesty, or learning of the proposing layer. We realize ATP in Mnemosyne, a runtime with an append-only transition log, effective-state projection, dependency-safe compensation, and active commitment records, and prove four safety properties relative to C (authority separation, serial-equivalent generative admission, evidence-preserving repair, and obligation containment) together with a bounded-reactive-repair guarantee for its localized repair protocol (LCRP). A reproducible artifact rejects the targeted violations across nine falsification tests while still admitting valid work, at under 6% projection-and-validation overhead, and bounded local repair edits an order of magnitude fewer operations than global recompute. Mnemosyne is open source: https://github.com/eyuchang/Mnemosyne/tree/arxiv-atp-rq1-rq9b-r8-v2.

</details>


### [206] [Managed Autonomy at Runtime: Gear-Based Safety and Governance for Single- and Multi-Agent Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00334)
*Srini Ramaswamy,Wang Miaosheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为\system{}的离散时间控制系统，通过组合五种执行齿轮（\Gobs{}, \Gsug{}, \Gplan{}, \Gexec{}, \Gint{}）和效用门控调度与事件驱动回退机制，解决自主智能体在缺乏人类持续监督时的安全问题。单智能体场景证明了单调稳定性、执行安全性、最终稳定性、回退完备性和马氏决策过程等价性。多智能体CPS场景应用\smart{}生命周期管理，通过共识门控、群体Lyapunov分析等实现分布式安全保证。在三台UR5机器人装配单元上测试，异常检测率达99.6%，检测延迟降低3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体（包括LLM驱动的软件智能体和机器人物理智能体）在缺乏人类持续监督时面临三类常见故障：未验证动作导致的安全违规、无约束循环导致的行为不稳定、未处理错误状态导致的连续性损失。现有方法缺乏形式化的安全保证机制。

Method: 提出\system{}离散时间控制系统，包含：(1)五种执行齿轮（观察、建议、规划、执行、干预）作为微观权限；(2)效用门控调度与事件驱动回退机制；(3)单智能体形式化证明（单调稳定性、执行安全性、最终稳定性等）；(4)多智能体CPS中应用\smart{}管理生命周期，结合共识门控、群体Lyapunov分析、每智能体齿轮权限和会合控制。

Result: 在三台UR5机器人装配单元上，基于NIST数据集校准的故障幅度，进行10,000次蒙特卡洛实验。\system{}达到99.6%异常检测率（单智能体基线仅2.1%），检测延迟降低3.5倍，并提供形式化物理工作空间安全证书。

Conclusion: 执行齿轮作为\smart{}运行时治理状态下的微观权限，实现了动作控制与自主治理的分离，为安全关键型自主系统提供了形式化保证框架。

Abstract: Autonomous agents, whether LLM-driven software agents or robotic physical agents, face a common class of failure modes when operating without continuous human oversight: safety violations from unverified actions, behavioral instability from unconstrained loops, and continuity loss from unhandled error states. We develop \system{}, a discrete-time control system that combines five execution gears (\Gobs{}, \Gsug{}, \Gplan{}, \Gexec{}, \Gint{}) with utility-gated dispatch and event-driven fallback. For the single-agent case, we prove monotonic stability, execution safety, eventual stabilization, fallback completeness, and equivalence to a gear-constrained Markov decision process. For multi-agent cyber-physical systems (CPS), we apply the established \smart{} managed-autonomy lifecycle and map runtime evidence into its four governance states (\Stable{}/\Meta{}/\Assisted{}/\Regulated{}). Consensus gating, swarm-level Lyapunov analysis, per-agent gear authority, and rendezvous control provide distributed safety and stability guarantees, including zero collision under the stated assumptions. We evaluate the resulting runtime on a three-agent UR5 robotic assembly cell using fault magnitudes calibrated from the NIST \emph{Degradation Measurement of Robot Arm Position Accuracy} dataset across 10,000 Monte Carlo episodes. It achieves a 99.6\% anomaly detection rate versus 2.1\% for the single-agent baseline, reduces detection latency by $3.5\times$, and supplies a formal physical-workspace safety certificate. The execution gears act as micro-level permissions beneath the \smart{} runtime governance states, separating action control from autonomy governance.

</details>


### [207] [Personalization as Inverse Planning: Learning Latent Design Intents for Agentic Slide Generation via Structural Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00407)
*Tianci Liu,Zihan Dong,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,jing Gao,Emre Kiciman,Ranveer Chandra,Wei-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SPIRE框架，将页面级幻灯片个性化（PSP）形式化为逆向规划问题，通过结构化去噪和强化学习双智能体协作，在不依赖具体工具知识的情况下实现精细化的幻灯片设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI代理方法依赖预设模板或用户详细指令，无法捕捉细粒度、页面级别的潜在设计意图，导致页面级幻灯片个性化（PSP）问题未得到解决。

Method: 将PSP形式化为逆向规划问题，提出SPIRE框架：通过故意破坏干净幻灯片的视觉结构创建可验证的去噪任务，两个智能体通过强化学习（RL）协作学习优化可执行设计。理论上证明了结构化去噪是PSP的一致替代目标，且多智能体公式能严格降低RL中的策略梯度方差。

Result: 广泛实验表明SPIRE在页面级幻灯片个性化任务上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SPIRE通过结构化去噪和双智能体强化学习，有效解决了无需工具知识的页面级幻灯片个性化难题，在理论和实验上均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Slide design requires personalizing both deck themes and page layouts. Yet, current AI agent-based methods struggle with fine-grained, page-level design. Solely relying on prespecified templates or user verbose instructions, they fail to capture latent design intents, leaving Page-level Slide Personalization (PSP) unresolved. To close this gap, this work formulates PSP as an inverse planning problem. We propose to learn a design intent without assuming any knowledge of the specific executing tools (e.g., PowerPoint, Beamer) being used. However, relinquishing control over these tools makes the problem intractable to optimize end-to-end. To overcome this, we propose SPIRE, a principled framework to solve PSP approximately. By intentionally corrupting the visual structures of clean slides, SPIRE creates a verifiable task to denoise the corruption, whereby two agents learn to collaboratively refine executable designs via reinforcement learning (RL). We present a proof that structural denoising is a consistent surrogate for PSP, and that the multi-agent formulation strictly reduces policy gradient variance in RL. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of SPIRE.

</details>


### [208] [PHREEQC-MCQ-200: A Diagnostic Benchmark for Tool-Augmented Scientific Simulator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00436)
*Ke Zhang,Sahchit Chundur,Mohammad Javad Qomi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PHREEQC-MCQ-200基准，评估工具增强型智能体在确定性地球化学模拟中的表现，发现工具访问虽整体提升准确率，但存在非单调收益（导致部分原正确题目答错），且输出访问协议对性能有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究工具接入何时能让科学计算更可靠而非更复杂，揭示工具增强型智能体的非单调性能变化及输出协议影响。

Method: 构建PHREEQC-MCQ-200基准（200道选择题，源自21个已验证的PHREEQC场景），要求智能体构建输入、执行模拟、检查输出并提交答案，测试不同模型族的工具增强表现。

Result: 工具访问整体提升准确率，但存在个别题目从正确变为错误的回归现象；表格目录式输出接口可降低较弱模型的token开销并提升准确率，但会降低中等模型的性能。

Conclusion: 科学工具使用应视为端到端诊断问题，评估应报告准确率、逐题保留率、输出访问敏感性、轨迹失败及计算链断裂点等指标。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly connected to scientific software, yet it remains unclear when tool access makes scientific computation more reliable rather than merely more complex. We introduce PHREEQC-MCQ-200, a benchmark for evaluating tool-augmented agents on deterministic aqueous-geochemistry simulations. The benchmark contains 200 multiple-choice questions derived from 21 validated PHREEQC scenarios, requiring agents to construct simulator inputs, execute PHREEQC, inspect structured outputs, and commit to final answers.
  Across multiple frontier and mid-tier model families, simulator access substantially improves aggregate accuracy, confirming that grounded execution is necessary for many scientific-computation tasks. However, the gains are not monotonic: tool-augmented agents also lose items they answered correctly without tools, revealing regressions that average accuracy alone hides. We further show that output-access protocol matters. A table-of-contents interface can reduce token cost while preserving or improving accuracy for stronger models, but it degrades performance for mid-tier models that cannot reliably navigate structured simulator outputs.
  PHREEQC-MCQ-200 therefore frames scientific tool use as an end-to-end diagnostic problem rather than a simple tool-calling capability. We argue that evaluations of scientific agents should report not only accuracy, but also item-level retention, output-access sensitivity, trajectory failures, and where the computation chain breaks.

</details>


### [209] [Agri-SAGE: Simulation-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM for Context-Aware Agricultural Advisory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00454)
*Vedant Balasubramaniam,Geetha Charan,Manojkumar Patil,Rohit P Suresh,V Priyanka,Kodur Sai Vinay Sathvik,Y. Narahari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agri-SAGE是一个将多智能体LLM推理与APSIM生物物理模拟相结合的闭环框架，通过检索增强生成和模拟验证，解决了传统农业咨询系统静态指南与LLM生成建议不可靠的双重问题。实验表明Tree of Thoughts实现最高产量，Reflexion以更低计算成本达到相近效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统农业咨询系统的静态指南无法适应季节内动态变化和不确定性，而当前基于LLM的咨询系统虽能生成农艺上可信的建议，但在生理学层面缺乏说服力。两种范式存在根本性矛盾，亟需一种既能利用LLM的推理能力又能通过生物物理模拟验证建议有效性的框架。

Method: 提出Agri-SAGE闭环框架，核心包含两个组件：1) 检索增强的多智能体LLM推理模块，用于生成农艺建议；2) APSIM生物物理仿真模块，用于验证建议的生理学合理性。在10年的回顾性分析中，评估了三种推理策略（Plan-and-Solve、Tree of Thoughts、Reflexion）的表现，并与静态PoP基线进行对比。

Result: 三种推理方法均显著优于静态PoP基线，其中Tree of Thoughts实现了最高的峰值产量；Reflexion通过利用跨季节的 episodic memory（情景记忆）在达到相近农艺效果的同时，大幅降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: Agri-SAGE框架有效解决了农业咨询系统中静态指南与LLM不可靠建议的双重局限，其中Tree of Thoughts追求最优产量，而Reflexion在效率与效果之间取得更优平衡，为实际部署提供了灵活选择。

Abstract: Agricultural advisory systems face a fundamental tension: static agronomic guidelines offer consistent, evidence-based recommendations, yet remain blind to in-season variability and dynamic uncertainties. Recent advisory systems powered by LLMs are liable for a different risk of generating recommendations that are agronomically credible but physiologically unconvincing. Agri-SAGE is a closed-loop framework designed to resolve the above two limitations by integrating retrieval-grounded multi-agent LLM reasoning with APSIM-based biophysical simulation, to generate and validate agronomic advisories. To assess this framework, we evaluate three reasoning approaches, namely Plan-and-Solve, Tree of Thoughts, and Reflexion, over a 10-year retrospective analysis. All three significantly outperform static PoP (Package-of-Practice) baselines, with Tree of Thoughts achieving impressive peak yields. At the same time, Reflexion achieves comparable agronomic outcomes at substantially lower computational cost by leveraging cross-seasonal episodic memory.

</details>


### [210] [Multi-scale Mixture of World Models for Embodied Agents in Evolving Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00457)
*Jinwoo Jang,Daniel J. Rho,Sihyung Yoon,Hyunsuk Cho,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MuSix提出了一种面向具身智能体的尺度感知混合专家框架，通过两阶段路由机制（元路由+基础路由）和尺度依赖的遗忘率，实现了多尺度推理与动态知识适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE在具身智能场景中存在两个问题：路由缺乏明确的尺度概念，无法在特定尺度进行针对性更新；统一的更新策略无法适应不同尺度知识过时的速度差异。

Method: 提出MuSix框架：(1) 两阶段路由机制——元路由基于体验距离（受建构水平理论启发）映射到连续尺度空间权重，然后每尺度基路由选择具体世界模型；(2) 尺度依赖的遗忘率——低尺度知识快速刷新，高尺度抽象长期保持；(3) 门控跨尺度转移机制维持层级一致性。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench和HAZARD基准上，MuSix在多尺度推理和动态适应方面优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模尺度感知的混合与演化机制，MuSix有效解决了具身智能体在多尺度推理和知识适应方面的关键挑战。

Abstract: Embodied agents operating in the real world require multi-scale reasoning and knowledge adaptation as conditions change. We identify two challenges in applying Mixture of Experts (MoE) to this setting: routing lacks an explicit notion of scale, preventing targeted updates at specific scales, and a uniform update policy cannot accommodate the different rates at which knowledge at each scale becomes outdated. We present MuSix, a framework that addresses both challenges through scale-aware world model mixture and evolution. A two-stage routing mechanism grounds scale selection in experiential distance, a measure of situational novelty inspired by Construal Level Theory: a meta-router first maps this quantity to a weight over continuous scale space, then per-scale base routers select world models within the identified scale. For adaptation, scale-dependent forgetting rates allow low-scale knowledge to refresh rapidly while high-scale abstractions persist, and gated inter-scale transfer maintains coherence across the hierarchy. Experiments on EmbodiedBench and HAZARD show that MuSix improves over state-of-the-art baselines on multi-scale reasoning and dynamic adaptation.

</details>


### [211] [AI Native Games: A Survey and Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00527)
*Zhiyue Xu,Fandi Meng,Kaijie Xu,Clark Verbrugge,Simon Lucas,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了"AI原生游戏"的概念——即运行时生成式AI构成核心循环不可或缺的部分，并提出G/N双轴分类法，对53款游戏进行了分析，探讨了将语义开放性组织为稳定游戏机制的设计挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏虽广泛使用生成式AI，但缺乏对"AI原生"的明确定义。作者希望通过反事实准则区分真正的AI原生游戏与AI增强游戏，为这一新兴领域建立分析框架。

Method: 提出反事实准则（移除AI后核心玩法是否崩溃）定义AI原生游戏；构建G/N双轴分类法（G轴：玩家面向的游戏类型；N轴：使AI不可替代的核心机制）；对53款公开游戏与原型进行筛选和分析。

Result: 发现当前AI原生游戏集中于语言优先的设计（如叙事冒险、认知交互、生成式叙事），而语义裁决、多智能体模拟、生成式构建、关系/伙伴型玩法等类别仍较少被探索。

Conclusion: AI原生设计的核心挑战是将语义开放性组织为稳定游戏机制，依赖机械不变量（目标、规则、状态、反馈、节奏、玩家能动性）来使开放式的AI输出可解释且有意义。最后提出可控生成、AI即机制设计、多模态与多智能体系统、推理经济学、评估、安全与监管等未来方向。

Abstract: Generative AI now enables games to produce dialogue, quests, characters, images, and worlds at runtime. Yet generation alone does not make a game AI-native, nor does it guarantee playability. This paper defines AI-native games by whether runtime generative AI is constitutive of the core loop: if the AI component were removed or trivially replaced, the central form of play would collapse or become fundamentally different. This counterfactual criterion separates AI-native games from AI-augmented games, boundary artifacts, chatbots, tavern-style role-play, procedural content generation, and AI-assisted production. Using this definition, we screen candidate artifacts and analyze 53 publicly available AI-native games and prototypes. We introduce a dual-axis G/N taxonomy: the G-axis captures player-facing game type, while the N-axis captures the dominant AI mechanic that makes generative AI indispensable to play. The corpus is concentrated around language-forward designs, especially narrative adventure, epistemic interaction, and generative narrative, while categories such as semantic adjudication, multi-agent simulation, generative construction, and relationship/companion play remain less represented. We argue that the central design problem is organizing semantic openness into stable gameplay. AI-native design depends on mechanical invariants: goals, rules, state, feedback, pacing, and player agency that make open-ended AI outputs interpretable and consequential. We conclude with a roadmap for controllable generation, AI-as-mechanic design, multimodal and multi-agent systems, inference economics, evaluation, safety, and regulation.

</details>


### [212] [HARC: Coupling Harmfulness and Refusal Directions for Robust Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00572)
*Shei Pern Chua,Fangzhao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现对齐LLM中的安全表示：越狱通过抑制拒绝/有害方向实现，响应位置仍能识别有害内容。提出HARC微调方法，在有害-拒绝子空间进行耦合干预，在五个模型家族上实现了最优的鲁棒性-能力-可用性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对齐LLM内部如何表示安全信息，诊断对齐漏洞，解释越狱为何成功，并指导设计更鲁棒的安全对齐策略。

Method: 1) 分析prompt侧和response侧token位置的有害性( harmfulness)和拒绝(refusal)方向；2) 提出HARC(Harmfulness-And-Refusal Coupling)微调方法，在有害-拒绝子空间内对prompt和response两个位置进行方向耦合干预，不破坏残差流其余部分。

Result: 1) 越狱通过抑制拒绝或有害方向成功；2) 模型在生成响应时仍能识别有害内容，即使未在prompt侧识别；3) HARC在六个基线方法中实现最优的鲁棒性-能力-可用性权衡；4) 安全方向在五个模型家族和两种规模上可迁移。

Conclusion: HARC方法在不损害通用能力和增加过度拒绝的情况下，有效提升了LLM的安全对齐鲁棒性，且安全方向可跨模型迁移。

Abstract: Understanding how aligned LLMs internally represent safety is critical for diagnosing alignment vulnerabilities, as it explains why jailbreaks succeed and informs the design of robust alignment strategies. Prior work shows that aligned LLMs encode harmfulness and refusal as separable directions in the residual stream at prompt-side token positions. We show that jailbreaks succeed at prompt encoding by suppressing either the refusal or harmfulness direction before any token is generated, with distinct attack classes occupying separable regions of the harmfulness-refusal plane. Extending the analysis to response-token positions, we find that the model recognizes harmful content while it is generating that content, even when it failed to recognize the input as harmful at the prompt side. Motivated by our findings, we introduce HARC (Harmfulness-And-Refusal Coupling), a fine-tuning method that pairs the two directions across both prompt and response positions. Since the intervention is confined to the harmfulness-refusal subspace, it leaves the rest of the residual stream intact and does not degrade general capability or inflate over-refusal. Across extensive experiments, HARC achieves the strongest robustness-capability-usability trade-off among six baselines spanning the major training-time and inference-time safety methods. The harmfulness and refusal directions at prompt and response positions transfer across the five model families and two scales we tested without architecture-specific tuning.

</details>


### [213] [AGI Maze as a Benchmark Framework for World-Modeling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00627)
*Alexey Potapov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGI Maze框架，用于测试大语言模型在部分可观测、有状态环境中的推理能力。实验发现，即使是简单迷宫，标准LLM也无法在推理时内部表示世界状态，而基于消息历史的智能体虽有所提升，但仍远不如人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的默认next-token预测模式无法可靠生成持久、可操作的外部世界表征，尤其在部分可观测、有状态且需要记忆和隐状态假设的任务中表现不佳。现有基准缺乏此类轻量级、无需高维感知输入的测试环境。

Method: 构建AGI Maze框架——一系列基于网格的迷宫任务，提供简洁API和多种难度级别。评估若干vanilla LLM在简单迷宫上的表现，并引入一个允许使用消息历史作为工作记忆的基线智能体。

Result: 标准LLM在推理时无法内部表示迷宫状态；允许使用消息历史的智能体虽有性能提升，但在远超人类所需的步数预算内仍无法可靠解决小规模迷宫。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在需要世界状态表征和记忆的推理任务上存在根本性缺陷，AGI Maze可作为衡量智能体世界建模能力的有效基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are powerful pattern-completion systems, but their default operating mode - predicting the next token from a static context - does not reliably produce persistent, manipulable representations of an external world. Many tasks that look like "reasoning" in text become substantially harder once the environment is partially observable, stateful, and requires memory and structured hypotheses about hidden state. AGI Maze is a lightweight framework for building such environments without requiring high-dimensional sensory inputs. It provides a family of grid-based maze tasks with a clean API and multiple difficulty regimes. The goal is to create benchmarks where agents must learn and use world state representations, not just infer a local rule over readily provided observations. We provide an initial evaluation of several vanilla LLMs on simple mazes showing that they fail to represent mazes internally at LLM inference time. We also introduce a baseline agent, which is allowed to use its message history as a working memory to construct descriptions of observations at agentic runtime. Although this can improve performance, it is still insufficient for an LLM agent to reliably solve even small mazes within a step budget that is more than enough for humans.

</details>


### [214] [Coachable agents for interactive gameplay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00642)
*Roberto Capobianco,Harm van Seijen,Nolan D. Bard,Neil Burch,Fatima Davelouis,Josh Davidson,Alisa Devlic,Yunshu Du,Ishan Durugkar,Siddhant Gangapurwala,Daniel Hernandez,G. Zacharias Holland,Sahil Jain,Kenta Kawamoto,Raksha Kumaraswamy,Patrick MacAlpine,Dustin R. Morrill,Declan Oller,Francesco Riccio,Akanksha Saran,Craig Sherstan,Kaushik Subramanian,Thomas J. Walsh,Samuel Barrett,Kizza N. Frisbee,Mady Govil,Johannes Günther,Varun R. Kompella,James A. MacGlashan,Maxwell Svetlik,Michael D. Thomure,Jaden B. Travnik,Kevin Waugh,Elahe Aghapour,Florian Fuchs,Andreanne Lemay,Shruti Mishra,Takuma Seno,Peter Stone,Michael Spranger,Peter R. Wurman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合UVFA、特定训练场景、学习算法和数据增强的框架，使强化学习智能体能够实时展现出不同的"风格"行为，并在《地平线：西之绝境》、《GT赛车》和人形机器人等不同领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习通常只学习单一的最优行为，但在许多实际应用中，用户希望在运行时对任务的执行方式（即"风格"）进行实时控制，而现有方法缺乏这种灵活性。

Method: 将通用价值函数逼近器（UVFA）与精心设计的训练场景、学习算法和数据增强相结合，构建一个能够体现不同风格的智能体训练框架。

Result: 在《地平线：西之绝境》、《GT赛车》和人形机器人测试域中，智能体在保持主任务性能的同时，对风格指令表现出强烈的一致性。

Conclusion: 该框架允许终端用户在运行时灵活选择最终行为，提供了对智能体执行表现的可控性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has proven to be a valuable tool in the creation of advanced AI and robotic systems, contributing to everything from game playing to robotics to foundation models. Through trial-and-error, these AI systems typically learn one, near-optimal behavior to solve their tasks. However, there are many use cases in which one would like to assert some level of control, preferably in real time, over how the task is solved. We refer to these modifications of a core task as styles. We combine universal value function approximators (UVFAs) with carefully selected training scenarios, learning algorithms, and data augmentation to create a framework for coaching agents that exhibit styles in complex domains. We demonstrate the framework's application in the AAA video games Horizon Forbidden West and Gran Turismo, and in an open-source humanoid test domain. Despite the different nature of the domains -- car racing, stylized game combat, and humanoid walking -- each agent shows strong coherence to the style requests while still satisfying the main task in its domain. Importantly, the techniques outlined in this paper allow an end user to choose the final behavior at run time, giving them flexible control over the final executed performance.

</details>


### [215] [Self-GC: Self-Governing Context for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00692)
*Xubin Hao,Hongjin Meng,Xin Yin,Jiawei Zhu,Chenpeng Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Self-GC将LLM智能体的上下文管理从被动文本清理转变为主动的对象生命周期治理，通过对用户轮次、工具调用和技能状态进行索引，并引入侧通道规划器执行折叠、掩码和剪枝操作，在多个评测集上显著优于启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程LLM智能体系统在处理结构化上下文（工具结果、文件、计划、用户约束）时，依赖启发式剪枝或最终摘要，但启发式方法忽略未来依赖，摘要方法会丢失精确证据和可编辑工件，缺乏系统化的上下文生命周期管理。

Method: Self-GC将用户轮次、工具调用片段和技能状态转化为索引对象；通过侧通道规划器提出fold（折叠）、mask（掩码）和prune（剪枝）动作；并由执行框架实施可恢复的sidecar、安全的提交边界和缓存感知的提交机制。

Result: 在33会话Hard Set上剪枝43.95%前缀token且84.85%未来续写不受影响（基线54.55%-69.70%）；在332会话套件上no-impact率达91.27%-94.58%（基线77.71%-87.46%）；生产环境白天平均输入token减少10%-15%，峰值接近20%。

Conclusion: 上下文管理应被视为对可索引、可恢复对象的运行时生命周期控制，而非事后文本清理，Self-GC为此提供了有效的实现框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate tool results, files, plans, and user constraints that are too structured to be treated as a disposable text suffix. Current systems mostly rely on in-run heuristics such as chronological pruning and tool-output masking, or on final self-summary near a context limit. Heuristics are cheap but blind to future dependencies; summaries preserve narrative state but often hide exact evidence, locators, and editable artifacts. We present Self-GC, where GC denotes self-governing context while deliberately echoing garbage collection: the system does not merely reclaim unused tokens, but governs the lifecycle of agent context objects. Self-GC turns user turns, tool spans, and skill state into indexed objects; asks a side-channel planner to propose fold, mask, and prune actions; and lets the harness enforce recoverable sidecars, safe commit boundaries, and cache-aware commit. On a 33-session Hard Set, Self-GC prunes 43.95% of prefix tokens while leaving 84.85% of future continuations unaffected, compared with no-impact rates of 54.55% to 69.70% for heuristic baselines. On a 332-session production-derived suite, three planner backbones reach no-impact rates of 91.27% to 94.58%, while baselines remain at 77.71% to 87.46%. In production, an online account-level split reduces daytime average input tokens by 10% to 15%, with peak reductions near 20%. These results point to context management as runtime lifecycle control over indexed, recoverable objects rather than post hoc text cleanup.

</details>


### [216] [Self-Evolving Agents with Anytime-Valid Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00871)
*Biswa Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SEA架构，通过冻结基础模型+可审计门控机制实现安全的自演进，在SWE-bench上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演进智能体违反了大多数学习理论保证的假设——数据、评估器、组件和假设空间都是由正在更新的策略产生的，需要一种可控的自修改架构。

Method: SEA架构：将自修改限制在小型转向适配器和带版本控制的冻结基础模型外壳上，通过随时可验证的门控机制（带有错误预算的审计证书）批准每次修改。使用五种循环控制器组合已有保证，结合五种验证器循环机制（Best-of-N、微步搜索、自编复制预言机、搜索层控制、自修复）提供密集信号。

Result: 在52个SWE-bench Verified实例上，基础能力是主导因素；在两个强基础模型上，无操作对照实验显示该套件贡献+4和+5（GLM 5.2: 24→28；GPT: 29→34，即65%最佳），事件日志确认机制触发并防止了性能回退。

Conclusion: SEA架构能安全地实现自演进，但结果基于单次运行昂贵评估，确认运行间方差和适应每任务算法组合是未来工作。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents violate the assumption behind most learning-theoretic guarantees: the data, evaluator, components, and hypothesis space are produced by the policy being updated. We present \textbf{SEA}, an architecture that confines self-modification to a small steering adapter and a versioned harness around a \emph{frozen} base model and admits each modification only through an anytime-valid gate that emits an auditable certificate against a fixed error budget. Five loop controllers compose published guarantees; because such gates can only \emph{select} among behaviors the frozen base already produces, five verifier-in-the-loop mechanisms -- best-of-$N$, micro-step search, self-authored reproduction oracles, search-layer control, and self-repair -- supply the dense, grader-free signal the gates require, computed from the issue text alone. On a $52$-instance SWE-bench Verified subset across four base models, base capability is the dominant, confound-free effect, and on two strong base models a deliberate no-op-composite control isolates the suite's contribution at $+4$ and $+5$ (\textsc{Glm}~5.2 $24\to28$; \textsc{Gpt} $29\to34$, the $65\%$ best), with event logs confirming that its mechanisms fire and prevent regressions. Results are single-run on expensive evaluations; confirming run-to-run variance and adapting the per-task algorithm mix are future work.

</details>


### [217] [Two AI Metrics Diverged: Will it Make All the Difference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00913)
*Alex Fogelson,Zachary A. Brown,Hans Gundlach,Jayson Lynch,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了当AI模型的计算规模指数级增长时，前沿模型的能力是否会超越小预算开发者可获取的"温顺模型"（meek models）。作者发现答案取决于如何衡量AI能力：在验证损失等有界指标上差距缩小，但在其他无界指标上前沿模型领先优势持续扩大。最终，有界指标有利于能力普及，无界指标则导致能力集中在少数富裕的参与者手中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI计算规模指数级增长，一个关键问题浮现：前沿模型是否会因成本过高而超出小规模开发者的可及范围，还是能力会收敛到"温顺模型"上？作者希望理清这一问题的答案，为AI政策制定提供理论基础。

Method: 基于Gundlach等人(2025b)的工作，作者将性能指标按其与训练/推理计算量的函数关系进行分类，提供了严格的数学条件来确定哪些指标有利于"温顺模型"（有界指标），并指出许多常见有界指标存在对应的无界指标（反之亦然）。

Result: 验证损失显示差距缩小，但其他指标上前沿模型领先优势持续扩大；有界指标总是有利于温顺模型；许多常见有界/无界指标存在密切对应的镜像指标；有界与无界指标可能得出相反的政策建议。

Conclusion: 特定能力（如软件工程、合成生物学、修辞说服力）若在关键维度上是无界的，能力将集中在少数富裕参与者手中；若是有界的，能力将通过温顺模型广泛普及。因此，领域内选取恰当的度量指标是政策制定的前提。

Abstract: As exponential compute scaling continues, will the capabilities of frontier AI models outstrip what is accessible to developers on a small fixed budget? Or will capabilities converge, with "meek models inheriting the earth"? Building on Gundlach et al. (2025b), we show that the answer depends on how we value and measure AI capabilities. We discuss conventional performance measures and show that, while validation loss shows a shrinking gap, on other metrics frontier models grow their lead forever. Classifying performance metrics by their functional forms in relation to training (and inference) compute, we provide tight mathematical conditions for determining which metrics favor meek models, and show that bounded performance metrics always do. But careful interpretation of performance metrics is essential: we show that many common bounded metrics have closely-related counterpart metrics that are unbounded (and vice versa). Determining the apt metric in a domain is a prerequisite for policy, since bounded and unbounded metrics may suggest opposing policy responses. If a particular capability -- like software engineering, synthetic biology, or rhetorical persuasiveness -- is unbounded when measured in the terms we care about, frontier-level capability will likely be concentrated in the hands of a few wealthy actors. Conversely, if that capability is instead bounded, frontier-level capabilities proliferate through meek models into the hands of the many.

</details>


### [218] [Graph-Native Reinforcement Learning Enables Traceable Scientific Hypothesis Generation through Conceptual Recombination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00924)
*Subhadeep Pal,Shashwat Sourav,Tirthankar Ghosal,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-PRefLexOR，一种图原生推理模型，通过GRPO强化学习将推理组织为显式阶段，在100个材料科学问题上相比基线模型提升40-65%，推理可追溯性显著增强，语义多样性提高2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准大语言模型面对开放式材料设计问题时，虽然生成结果流畅，但中间推理过程难以追溯，无法判断最终答案是否建立在连贯推理之上。因此需要一种能显式组织多步推理、具有可追溯性的AI系统来加速材料发现。

Method: 提出Graph-PRefLexOR模型家族，采用GRPO强化学习微调，将推理过程显式分为四个阶段：机制探索、图构建、模式提取和假设合成。通过将神经语言生成与符号化关系结构（图结构）结合，使得因果连接可构建、可检查和可重用。

Result: 在100个材料科学与力学文献的开放性问题中，相比基线模型提升40-65%，推理可追溯性增益最大。嵌入分析显示语义探索范围更广，语义多样性约为基线2-3倍。语义回溯和逐层隐藏状态分析显示结构化推理与最终答案之间的对齐更强。测试时图扩展发现，额外计算主要增加有界语义空间内的长程概念重组。

Conclusion: 图原生强化学习是实现可解释AI系统、用于科学假设生成的有效路径，可推广至材料设计及其他科学领域。

Abstract: Accelerating materials discovery requires AI systems that can generate scientifically valid hypotheses through multi-step, domain-grounded reasoning. Standard large language models often produce fluent but weakly traceable responses to open-ended materials design problems, making it difficult to determine whether final answers are supported by coherent intermediate reasoning. We develop Graph-PRefLexOR, a family of graph-native reasoning models fine-tuned with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to organize reasoning into explicit phases for mechanism exploration, graph construction, pattern extraction, and hypothesis synthesis. This design links neural language generation with symbolic relational structure, enabling causal connections to be constructed, inspected, and reused. On 100 open-ended questions from materials science and mechanics literature, Graph-PRefLexOR achieves 40-65% improvements over corresponding base models, with the largest gains in reasoning traceability. Embedding analyses show broader semantic exploration and approximately 2-3 times greater semantic diversity than baselines. Semantic backtracking and layer-wise hidden-state analyses further show stronger alignment between structured reasoning and final answers. Finally, test-time graph expansion reveals that additional compute primarily increases long-range conceptual recombination within a bounded semantic space, rather than simply expanding semantic coverage. These results establish graph-native reinforcement learning as a pathway toward interpretable AI systems for scientific hypothesis generation in materials design and other scientific applications.

</details>


### [219] [Bayesian Uncertainty Propagation for Agentic RAG Pipelines: A Proof-of-Concept Study on Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00972)
*Louis Donaldson,Connor Walker,Koorosh Aslansefat,Yiannis Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向Agentic RAG系统的不确定性感知框架，通过贝叶斯网络传播规划器、评估器和生成器阶段的语义分歧与自评估信号，从而估计系统级不确定性并定位潜在失败点。在StrategyQA和HotpotQA上的实验表明，该方法在多跳推理场景下效果更佳，但在校准和信号可靠性方面仍存在局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic RAG系统的多阶段推理管道缺乏可靠的失败检测机制，导致高风险场景下难以保证可信部署。现有方法难以在系统层面量化不确定性和定位具体故障环节。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的Agentic RAG框架，包含规划器、评估器和生成器三个阶段，各阶段通过语义分歧和模型自评估产生不确定性信号，并利用贝叶斯网络传播这些信号，最终输出系统级不确定性估计和各节点级故障指示。

Result: 在HotpotQA上贝叶斯传播效果更优，不确定性在多跳推理中有效累积；StrategyQA上则暴露了信号校准不佳和上游信号不可靠的问题。评估指标包括AUROC、AUARC、ECE和Brier Score。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯不确定性传播是监控Agentic RAG系统的有前景但尚需改进的机制，未来需在工业领域（如海上风电维护决策支持）进行验证。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems requires mechanisms for estimating when multi-stage reasoning pipelines may fail. This paper presents an uncertainty-aware Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework in which planner, evaluator and generator stages produce uncertainty signals derived from semantic divergence and generator self-evaluation. These signals are propagated through a Bayesian Network (BN) to estimate system-level uncertainty and provide node-level indicators of potential failure points across the workflow. The approach is evaluated on StrategyQA and HotpotQA using GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4.1-Nano, with Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve (AUROC), Area Under the Accuracy-Rejection Curve (AUARC), Expected Calibration Error (ECE), and Brier Score used to assess discrimination, selective prediction and calibration. Results show that Bayesian propagation is more effective on HotpotQA, where uncertainty accumulates across multi-hop reasoning stages, while StrategyQA exposes limitations caused by miscalibration and unreliable upstream signals. The study positions Bayesian uncertainty propagation as a promising but preliminary mechanism for monitoring Agentic RAG systems, with future validation required in industrial domains such as Offshore Wind (OSW) maintenance decision support.

</details>


### [220] [PedNStream: Scalable Network Flow Simulation for Pedestrian Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01021)
*Weiming Mai,Dorine Duives,Serge Hoogendoorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PedNStream，一个基于 Link Transmission Model (LTM) 的开源宏观行人网络流仿真器，支持随机链路动力学、效用驱动的路径选择，并内置控制器接口，可高效实现大规模行人仿真与闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源行人仿真工具多为微观模型，难以兼顾计算效率与网络级闭环控制评估，缺乏适用于大规模人群管理与反馈控制的宏观仿真平台。

Method: 基于 Link Transmission Model (LTM) 扩展，引入随机链路动力学（扩散与活动诱导变异性），用效用函数替代动态用户均衡进行路径选择，采用模块化架构并内建控制器接口（门控、流分离、路径引导）。

Result: 合成场景验证了排队形成、回堵、拥堵消散与自适应路径调整等机制；真实网络实验验证了与实测行人计数的一致性；闭环案例展示了控制器集成能力；运行时分析量化了可扩展性。

Conclusion: PedNStream 是一个高效、实用的大规模行人网络仿真与控制测试平台。

Abstract: Large-scale crowd management requires pedestrian simulations that are both computationally efficient and compatible with feedback-based control. However, most open-source tools are either microscopic or not designed for network-scale closed-loop evaluation. This paper presents PedNStream (Pedestrian Network Flow Simulation), an open-source, Python-native simulator for macroscopic pedestrian network loading based on the Link Transmission Model (LTM). The framework extends LTM-based pedestrian models by incorporating stochastic link dynamics that capture diffusion and activity-induced variability, and replaces dynamic user equilibrium route choice with a utility-based formulation suited to uncertain, intervention-driven settings. PedNStream is implemented as a modular framework with built-in controller interfaces for interventions such as gating, flow separation, and route guidance. We evaluate the framework in a staged manner. Synthetic scenarios verify key mechanisms, including queue formation, spillback, congestion dissipation, and adaptive rerouting. Real-network experiments assess large-scale behavior and consistency with observed pedestrian counts. A closed-loop case study demonstrates controller integration, and a runtime analysis quantifies scalability. These results establish PedNStream as an efficient and practical testbed for large-scale pedestrian network simulation and control.

</details>


### [221] [Agentic generation of verifiable rules for deterministic, self-expanding reaction classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01061)
*Daniel Armstrong,Maarten Dobbelaere,Valentas Olikauskas,Helena Avila,Octavian Susanu,Jérôme Waser,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个全自动管道，利用多智能体大语言模型框架自动分类反应并生成反应规则，覆盖665,901个美国专利反应，将标准分类从68类扩展到14,073类，并以轻量级指纹分类器达到97.7%的准确率，实现了一个动态扩展的反应数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机辅助合成规划依赖人工编码的固定规则集，无法适应长尾化学分布和新化学反应，因此需要一种能够自动、可扩展地生成反应规则的方法。

Method: 采用多智能体LLM框架，在验证循环中自动分类反应并编写规则；对665,901个美国专利反应进行迭代，生成14,073类反应规则；使用轻量级指纹分类器进行推理。

Result: 将标准分类从68类扩展到14,073类；指纹分类器对未见反应分类准确率达97.7%，性能与领先的专有分类器相当且分辨率更高；能按需扩展到训练分布外的化学领域。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个活性的反应数据库，展示了一条将生成式模型转化为可靠、自扩展符号系统的通用路径。

Abstract: Computer-assisted synthesis planning breaks target molecules into accessible precursors using large libraries of reaction rules that assign each transformation a deterministic, interpretable label. But chemistry is long-tailed, making manual encoding intractable, and existing tools rely on fixed rulesets that cannot adapt to new chemistries.
